```
1→"""CLI entry point: python -m demo script.yaml [-o out.mp4] [--play] [--skin ares]."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→
    10→def main():
    11→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
    12→        prog="python -m demo",
    13→        description="Hermes Agent demo video framework",
    14→    )
    15→    parser.add_argument(
    16→        "script", help="Path to YAML screenplay file",
    17→    )
    18→    parser.add_argument(
    19→        "-o", "--output", type=Path, default=None,
    20→        help="Output video path (e.g. output.mp4). If omitted, defaults to --play mode.",
    21→    )
    22→    parser.add_argument(
    23→        "--play", action="store_true",
    24→        help="Preview in terminal (no recording)",
    25→    )
    26→    parser.add_argument(
    27→        "--skin", default=None,
    28→        help="Override skin name (e.g. ares, mono, slate)",
    29→    )
    30→    parser.add_argument(
    31→        "--typing-speed", type=float, default=None,
    32→        help="Override base typing speed (seconds per character)",
    33→    )
    34→    parser.add_argument(
    35→        "--timing", type=Path, default=None,
    36→        help="Write timing manifest JSON to this path (used internally by pipeline)",
    37→    )
    38→    parser.add_argument(
    39→        "--dry-run", action="store_true",
    40→        help="Print pipeline commands without executing them",
    41→    )
    42→    args = parser.parse_args()
    43→
    44→    # If neither --output nor --play, default to --play
    45→    if args.output is None and not args.play:
    46→        args.play = True
    47→
    48→    # Load screenplay
    49→    from demo.player import load_screenplay
    50→    screenplay = load_screenplay(
    51→        args.script,
    52→        skin_override=args.skin,
    53→        typing_speed_override=args.typing_speed,
    54→    )
    55→
    56→    if args.play:
    57→        # Terminal preview mode
    58→        from demo.player import play
    59→        try:
    60→            play(screenplay, timing_path=args.timing)
    61→        except KeyboardInterrupt:
    62→            sys.stdout.write(f"\033[0m\n")

    64→    else:

    72→            dry_run=args.dry_run,
    73→        )
    74→
    75→
```

```
1→"""Pydantic models for screenplay YAML and camera directives."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional, Union
     6→
     7→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
     8→
     9→
    10→# ── Camera ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    11→
    12→class CameraDirective(BaseModel):
    13→    """Camera position/zoom keyframe attached to a scene phase or standalone."""
    14→
    15→    zoom: float = 1.0
    16→    x: float = 0.5  # Normalized 0.0–1.0, viewport center
    17→    y: float = 0.5
    18→    at: str = "scene_start"  # Timing marker reference
    19→    duration: float = 0.5  # Transition time in seconds
    20→    ease: Literal["linear", "ease-in", "ease-out", "ease-in-out"] = "ease-in-out"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ── Tool entry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    24→
    25→class ToolEntry(BaseModel):
    26→    """A single tool progress line in a conversation scene."""
    27→
    28→    icon: str = "\u26a1"
    29→    verb: str = "tool"
    30→    detail: str = ""
    31→    duration: str = "0.5s"
    32→    delay: float = 0.15
    33→
    34→
    35→# ── Scene types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    36→
    37→class ConversationScene(BaseModel):
    38→    """A user→thinking→tools→response exchange."""
    39→
    40→    user: str = ""
    41→    tools: List[ToolEntry] = Field(default_factory=list)
    42→    response: str = ""
    43→    response_label: str = " \u2695 Hermes "
    44→    thinking_time: float = 2.0
    45→    typing_speed: Optional[float] = None
    46→    pause_between: Optional[float] = None
    47→    pre_pause: float = 0.5
    48→    post_pause: float = 0.5
    49→
    50→    # Camera directives (Phase 3)
    51→    camera: Optional[CameraDirective] = None
    52→    camera_response: Optional[CameraDirective] = None
    53→
    54→
    55→class ActionScene(BaseModel):
    56→    """A non-conversation action (clear, pause, print, banner, type_command, camera)."""
    57→
    58→    action: str
    59→    # pause
    60→    duration: Optional[float] = None
    61→    # print
    62→    text: Optional[str] = None
    63→    color: Optional[str] = None
    64→    # banner
    65→    model: Optional[str] = None
    66→    context: Optional[str] = None
    67→    session_id: Optional[str] = None
    68→    tools_count: Optional[int] = None
    69→    skills_count: Optional[int] = None
    70→    # type_command
    71→    command: Optional[str] = None
    72→    prefix: Optional[str] = None
    73→    output: Optional[str] = None
    74→    # camera (standalone)
    75→    zoom: Optional[float] = None
    76→    x: Optional[float] = None
    77→    y: Optional[float] = None
    78→    ease: Optional[str] = None
    79→
    80→
    81→Scene = Union[ConversationScene, ActionScene]
    82→
    83→
    84→# ── Output config ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    85→
    86→class OutputConfig(BaseModel):
    87→    """Rendering and encoding settings."""
    88→
    89→    width: int = 2560
    90→    height: int = 1440
    91→    final_width: int = 1280
    92→    final_height: int = 720
    93→    fps: int = 30
    94→    font_size: int = 22
    95→    font_family: str = "Menlo"
    96→
    97→
    98→# ── Top-level screenplay ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    99→
   100→class Screenplay(BaseModel):
   101→    """Complete screenplay document parsed from YAML."""
   102→
   103→    title: str = "Hermes Agent Demo"
   104→    skin: str = "default"
   105→    output: OutputConfig = Field(default_factory=OutputConfig)
   106→    typing_speed: float = 0.04
   107→    pause_between: float = 1.0
   108→    scenes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)
   109→
   110→    # ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   111→
   112→    def parsed_scenes(self) -> List[Scene]:
   113→        """Parse raw scene dicts into typed Scene models."""
   114→        result: List[Scene] = []
   115→        for raw in self.scenes:
   116→            if "action" in raw:
   117→                result.append(ActionScene(**raw))
   118→            else:
   119→                # Convert nested tool dicts to ToolEntry models
   120→                tools_raw = raw.get("tools", [])
   121→                tools = [ToolEntry(**t) if isinstance(t, dict) else t for t in tools_raw]
   122→                data = {**raw, "tools": tools}
   123→                # Parse camera sub-objects
   124→                if "camera" in data and isinstance(data["camera"], dict):
   125→                    data["camera"] = CameraDirective(**data["camera"])
   126→                if "camera_response" in data and isinstance(data["camera_response"], dict):
   127→                    data["camera_response"] = CameraDirective(**data["camera_response"])
   128→                result.append(ConversationScene(**data))
   129→        return result
   130→
```

```
1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""Hermes Agent demo player — plays scripted terminal sessions.
     3→
     4→Loads a YAML screenplay, parses it into typed scenes, and dispatches
     5→each scene to registered handlers via the scene registry.
     6→
     7→Supports skin engine integration for theming and emits a timing manifest
     8→for the camera post-processing pipeline.
     9→
    10→Usage:
    11→    python -m demo scripts/example.yaml --play
    12→    python -m demo scripts/example.yaml -o output.mp4
    13→    python demo/player.py demo/scripts/example.yaml   # legacy compat
    14→"""
    15→
    16→from __future__ import annotations
    17→
    18→import argparse
    19→import sys
    20→import time
    21→from pathlib import Path
    22→from typing import Optional
    23→
    24→import yaml
    25→from rich.console import Console
    26→
    27→from hermes_cli.skin_engine import load_skin
    28→
    29→from demo.schema import Screenplay
    30→from demo.scenes import (
    31→    PlaybackContext, TimingManifest, _RST,
    32→    dispatch, hex_to_ansi,
    33→)
    34→# Import scene modules to trigger @register decorators
    35→import demo.scenes.conversation  # noqa: F401
    36→import demo.scenes.action  # noqa: F401
    37→
    38→
    39→def load_screenplay(
    40→    path: str,
    41→    skin_override: Optional[str] = None,
    42→    typing_speed_override: Optional[float] = None,
    43→) -> Screenplay:
    44→    """Load and validate a YAML screenplay file."""
    45→    with open(path) as f:
    46→        raw = yaml.safe_load(f)
    47→
    48→    screenplay = Screenplay(**raw)
    49→
    50→    # Store source path for pipeline to reference
    51→    screenplay._source_path = Path(path).resolve()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    52→
    53→    if skin_override:
    54→        screenplay.skin = skin_override
    55→    if typing_speed_override is not None:
    56→        screenplay.typing_speed = typing_speed_override
    57→
    58→    return screenplay
    59→
    60→
    61→def play(
    62→    screenplay: Screenplay,
    63→    timing_path: Optional[Path] = None,
    64→) -> None:
    65→    """Play a screenplay in the terminal.
    66→
    67→    Args:
    68→        screenplay: Parsed screenplay to play.
    69→        timing_path: If set, write timing manifest JSON here after playback.
    70→    """
    71→    skin = load_skin(screenplay.skin)
    72→    console = Console()
    73→
    74→    timing = TimingManifest() if timing_path else None
    75→
    76→    ctx = PlaybackContext(
    77→        skin=skin,
    78→        console=console,
    79→        typing_speed=screenplay.typing_speed,
    80→        pause_between=screenplay.pause_between,
    81→        recording_start=time.monotonic(),
    82→        timing=timing,
    83→    )
    84→
    85→    scenes = screenplay.parsed_scenes()
    86→
    87→    for i, scene in enumerate(scenes):
    88→        ctx.scene_index = i
    89→        dispatch(scene, ctx)
    90→
    91→    # Final prompt to show it's still "alive"
    92→    prompt_color = ctx.ansi_prompt
    93→    prompt_symbol = skin.get_branding("prompt_symbol", "❯ ")
    94→    sys.stdout.write(f"\n{prompt_color}{prompt_symbol}{_RST}")
    95→    sys.stdout.flush()
    96→    time.sleep(2)
    97→    sys.stdout.write("\n")
    98→
    99→    # Write timing manifest
   100→    if timing and timing_path:
   101→        timing.finalize()
   102→        timing.save(timing_path)
   103→
   104→
   105→# ── Legacy CLI entry point (python demo/player.py script.yaml) ─────────
   106→
   107→def main():
   108→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Hermes Agent demo player")
   109→    parser.add_argument("script", help="Path to YAML screenplay file")
   110→    parser.add_argument("--typing-speed", type=float, default=None,
   111→                        help="Base typing speed in seconds per character")
   112→    parser.add_argument("--skin", default=None, help="Skin name override")
   113→    args = parser.parse_args()
   114→
   115→    screenplay = load_screenplay(
   116→        args.script,
   117→        skin_override=args.skin,
   118→        typing_speed_override=args.typing_speed,
   119→    )
   120→
   121→    try:
   122→        play(screenplay)
   123→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   124→        sys.stdout.write(f"\n{_RST}")
   125→        sys.exit(0)
   126→
   127→
   128→if __name__ == "__main__":
   129→    main()
   130→
```

```
1→"""Orchestrates the full demo video pipeline: record → render → camera → encode."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Optional
     8→
     9→from demo.schema import Screenplay
    10→
    11→
    12→def run_pipeline(
    13→    screenplay: Screenplay,
    14→    output_path: Path,
    15→    skin_override: Optional[str] = None,
    16→    typing_speed_override: Optional[float] = None,
    17→    dry_run: bool = False,
    18→) -> None:
    19→    """Run the full recording + rendering pipeline.
    20→
    21→    Steps:
    22→    1. Record terminal session with asciinema (running player in --play mode)
    23→    2. Render .cast → GIF with agg at 2x resolution
    24→    3. Resolve camera keyframes from screenplay + timing manifest
    25→    4. Encode final video with ffmpeg (crop+scale for camera, or just scale)
    26→    """
    27→    from demo.camera import generate_ffmpeg_filter, resolve_keyframes
    28→    from demo.recorder import encode_ffmpeg, render_agg, record_asciinema, get_video_dimensions, get_video_duration
    29→
    30→    out = screenplay.output
    31→    work_dir = output_path.parent
    32→    stem = output_path.stem
    33→
    34→    cast_path = work_dir / f"{stem}.cast"
    35→    gif_path = work_dir / f"{stem}.gif"
    36→    timing_path = work_dir / f"{stem}_timing.json"
    37→
    38→    # Build the player command
    39→    player_cmd = [
    40→        sys.executable, "-m", "demo",
    41→        "--play",  # Terminal playback mode
    42→        "--timing", str(timing_path),
    43→    ]
    44→    if skin_override:
    45→        player_cmd.extend(["--skin", skin_override])
    46→    elif screenplay.skin != "default":
    47→        player_cmd.extend(["--skin", screenplay.skin])
    48→    if typing_speed_override:
    49→        player_cmd.extend(["--typing-speed", str(typing_speed_override)])
    50→
    51→    # We need the screenplay path — it's set by __main__ on the screenplay object
    52→    script_path = getattr(screenplay, "_source_path", None)
    53→    if script_path:
    54→        player_cmd.append(str(script_path))
    55→    else:
    56→        raise RuntimeError("Screenplay has no _source_path — cannot record")
    57→
    58→    # Calculate terminal size for recording
    59→    # Aim for ~200 cols to get good resolution at the configured font size
    60→    cols = 200
    61→    rows = 50
    62→
    63→    if dry_run:
    64→        _print_dry_run(
    65→            player_cmd, cast_path, gif_path, timing_path,
    66→            out, screenplay, output_path,
    67→        )
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    # Step 1: Record with asciinema
    71→    print(f"[1/4] Recording terminal session → {cast_path}")
    72→    record_asciinema(
    73→        command=player_cmd,
    74→        cast_path=cast_path,
    75→        cols=cols,
    76→        rows=rows,
    77→    )
    78→
    79→    # Step 2: Render to GIF with agg
    80→    print(f"[2/4] Rendering → {gif_path}")
    81→    render_agg(
    82→        cast_path=cast_path,
    83→        gif_path=gif_path,
    84→        font_size=out.font_size,
    85→        font_family=out.font_family,
    86→        fps=out.fps,
    87→    )
    88→
    89→    # Step 3: Resolve camera keyframes
    90→    video_filter = None
    91→    if timing_path.exists():
    92→        import json
    93→        from demo.scenes import TimingManifest, SceneTiming
    94→        timing_data = json.loads(timing_path.read_text())
    95→        manifest = TimingManifest(total_duration=timing_data.get("total_duration", 0))
    96→        for sd in timing_data.get("scenes", []):
    97→            st = SceneTiming(
    98→                index=sd["index"],
    99→                scene_type=sd["type"],
   100→                action=sd.get("action"),
   101→                start_t=sd["start_t"],
   102→                end_t=sd["end_t"],
   103→                markers=sd.get("markers", {}),
   104→            )
   105→            manifest.scenes.append(st)
   106→
   107→        print("[3/4] Resolving camera keyframes...")
   108→        keyframes = resolve_keyframes(screenplay, manifest)
   109→
   110→        # Get actual rendered dimensions and duration from the gif
   111→        try:
   112→            input_w, input_h = get_video_dimensions(gif_path)
   113→        except Exception:
   114→            input_w, input_h = out.width, out.height
   115→
   116→        try:
   117→            gif_duration = get_video_duration(gif_path)
   118→        except Exception:
   119→            gif_duration = manifest.total_duration
   120→
   121→        # Scale keyframe timestamps to match GIF timeline.
   122→        # The manifest uses monotonic clock from the player, but asciinema
   123→        # adds startup overhead and agg may adjust frame timing, so the
   124→        # GIF's actual duration differs from the manifest's.
   125→        if manifest.total_duration > 0 and abs(gif_duration - manifest.total_duration) > 0.5:
   126→            scale = gif_duration / manifest.total_duration
   127→            print(f"     Scaling keyframe times by {scale:.3f}x "
   128→                  f"(manifest={manifest.total_duration:.1f}s, gif={gif_duration:.1f}s)")
   129→            for kf in keyframes:
   130→                kf.t *= scale
   131→                kf.duration *= scale
   132→
   133→        video_filter = generate_ffmpeg_filter(
   134→            keyframes=keyframes,
   135→            input_w=input_w,
   136→            input_h=input_h,
   137→            output_w=out.final_width,
   138→            output_h=out.final_height,
   139→            total_duration=gif_duration,
   140→            fps=out.fps,
   141→        )
   142→    else:
   143→        print("[3/4] No timing manifest found, skipping camera...")
   144→        video_filter = f"scale={out.final_width}:{out.final_height}"
   145→
   146→    # Step 4: Encode final video
   147→    print(f"[4/4] Encoding → {output_path}")
   148→    encode_ffmpeg(
   149→        input_path=gif_path,
   150→        output_path=output_path,
   151→        video_filter=video_filter,
   152→        fps=out.fps,
   153→    )
   154→
   155→    print(f"Done! Output: {output_path}")
   156→
   157→
   158→def _print_dry_run(
   159→    player_cmd, cast_path, gif_path, timing_path,
   160→    out, screenplay, output_path,
   161→):
   162→    """Print the commands that would be run without executing them."""
   163→    print("=== DRY RUN ===\n")
   164→
   165→    print(f"Step 1: Record with asciinema")
   166→    print(f"  asciinema rec --overwrite --cols=200 --rows=50 \\")
   167→    print(f"    --command '{' '.join(player_cmd)}' \\")
   168→    print(f"    {cast_path}\n")
   169→
   170→    print(f"Step 2: Render with agg")
   171→    print(f"  agg --font-size={out.font_size} --font-family='{out.font_family}' \\")
   172→    print(f"    --fps-cap={out.fps} {cast_path} {gif_path}\n")
   173→
   174→    print(f"Step 3: Camera (resolve keyframes from {timing_path})")
   175→    scenes = screenplay.parsed_scenes()
   176→    camera_count = sum(
   177→        1 for s in scenes
   178→        if hasattr(s, "camera") and s.camera
   179→        or hasattr(s, "camera_response") and s.camera_response
   180→        or (hasattr(s, "action") and s.action == "camera")
   181→    )
   182→    print(f"  {camera_count} camera directive(s) found\n")
   183→
   184→    print(f"Step 4: Encode with ffmpeg")
   185→    print(f"  ffmpeg -y -i {gif_path} \\")
   186→    print(f"    -vf 'crop=...,scale={out.final_width}:{out.final_height}' \\")
   187→    print(f"    -c:v libx264 -preset slow -crf 18 -pix_fmt yuv420p \\")
```

```
1→"""Subprocess wrappers for asciinema recording and agg rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import shutil
     6→import subprocess
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import List, Optional
     9→
    10→
    11→def check_tool(name: str) -> Optional[str]:
    12→    """Return the path to a CLI tool, or None if not found."""
    13→    return shutil.which(name)
    14→
    15→
    16→def require_tool(name: str) -> str:
    17→    """Return the path to a CLI tool, or raise if not found."""
    18→    path = check_tool(name)
    19→    if path is None:
    20→        raise RuntimeError(
    21→            f"'{name}' not found on PATH. Install it first:\n"
    22→            f"  asciinema: pip install asciinema\n"
    23→            f"  agg: cargo install agg (or brew install agg)\n"
    24→            f"  ffmpeg: brew install ffmpeg"
    25→        )
    26→    return path
    27→
    28→
    29→# ── asciinema recording ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    30→
    31→def record_asciinema(
    32→    command: List[str],
    33→    cast_path: Path,
    34→    cols: int = 200,
    35→    rows: int = 50,
    36→    env: Optional[dict] = None,
    37→) -> None:
    38→    """Record a command with asciinema, producing a .cast file.
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        command: The command to record (e.g. ["python", "-m", "demo", "script.yaml", "--play"])
    42→        cast_path: Output path for the .cast file
    43→        cols: Terminal columns
    44→        rows: Terminal rows
    45→        env: Additional environment variables
    46→    """
    47→    asciinema = require_tool("asciinema")
    48→
    49→    cmd = [
    50→        asciinema, "rec",
    51→        "--overwrite",
    52→        f"--cols={cols}",
    53→        f"--rows={rows}",
    54→        "--command", " ".join(command),
    55→        str(cast_path),
    56→    ]
    57→
    58→    import os
    59→    run_env = dict(os.environ)
    60→    run_env["TERM"] = "xterm-256color"
    61→    if env:
    62→        run_env.update(env)
    63→
    64→    result = subprocess.run(
    65→        cmd,
    66→        env=run_env,
    67→        capture_output=True,
    68→        text=True,
    69→    )
    70→    if result.returncode != 0:
    71→        raise RuntimeError(
    72→            f"asciinema recording failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
    73→        )
    74→    if not cast_path.exists():
    75→        raise RuntimeError(f"asciinema did not produce output file: {cast_path}")
    76→
    77→
    78→# ── agg rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    79→
    80→def render_agg(
    81→    cast_path: Path,
    82→    gif_path: Path,
    83→    font_size: int = 22,
    84→    font_family: str = "JetBrains Mono",
    85→    cols: Optional[int] = None,
    86→    rows: Optional[int] = None,
    87→    fps: int = 30,
    88→) -> None:
    89→    """Render a .cast file to a GIF using agg.
    90→
    91→    Note: agg produces GIF. For high-quality video we use agg's raw frame
    92→    output or convert GIF → MP4 with ffmpeg.
    93→    """
    94→    agg = require_tool("agg")
    95→
    96→    cmd = [
    97→        agg,
    98→        f"--font-size={font_size}",
    99→        f"--font-family={font_family}",
   100→        f"--fps-cap={fps}",
   101→    ]
   102→    if cols:
   103→        cmd.append(f"--cols={cols}")
   104→    if rows:
   105→        cmd.append(f"--rows={rows}")
   106→
   107→    cmd.extend([str(cast_path), str(gif_path)])
   108→
   109→    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
   110→    if result.returncode != 0:
   111→        raise RuntimeError(
   112→            f"agg rendering failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
   113→        )
   114→
   115→
   116→# ── ffmpeg encoding ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   117→
   118→def encode_ffmpeg(
   119→    input_path: Path,
   120→    output_path: Path,
   121→    video_filter: Optional[str] = None,
   122→    fps: int = 30,
   123→) -> None:
   124→    """Encode video with ffmpeg, optionally applying a filter chain.
   125→
   126→    Handles both GIF→MP4 and raw frame→MP4 conversions.
   127→    """
   128→    ffmpeg = require_tool("ffmpeg")
   129→
   130→    cmd = [
   131→        ffmpeg, "-y",
   132→        "-i", str(input_path),
   133→    ]
   134→
   135→    if video_filter:
   136→        cmd.extend(["-vf", video_filter])
   137→
   138→    cmd.extend([
   139→        "-c:v", "libx264",
   140→        "-preset", "slow",
   141→        "-crf", "18",
   142→        "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
   143→        "-r", str(fps),
   144→        "-movflags", "+faststart",
   145→        str(output_path),
   146→    ])
   147→
   148→    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
   149→    if result.returncode != 0:
   150→        raise RuntimeError(
   151→            f"ffmpeg encoding failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
   152→        )
   153→
   154→
   155→def get_video_dimensions(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
   156→    """Get width and height of a video/gif file using ffprobe."""
   157→    ffprobe = require_tool("ffprobe")
   158→
   159→    result = subprocess.run(
   160→        [
   161→            ffprobe, "-v", "error",
   162→            "-select_streams", "v:0",
   163→            "-show_entries", "stream=width,height",
   164→            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0",
   165→            str(path),
   166→        ],
   167→        capture_output=True,
   168→        text=True,
   169→    )
   170→    if result.returncode != 0:
   171→        raise RuntimeError(f"ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
   172→
   173→    w, h = result.stdout.strip().split("x")
   174→    return int(w), int(h)
   175→
   176→
   177→def get_video_duration(path: Path) -> float:
   178→    """Get duration of a video/gif file in seconds using ffprobe."""
   179→    ffprobe = require_tool("ffprobe")
   180→
   181→    result = subprocess.run(
   182→        [
   183→            ffprobe, "-v", "error",
   184→            "-show_entries", "format=duration",
   185→            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0",
   186→            str(path),
   187→        ],
   188→        capture_output=True,
   189→        text=True,
   190→    )
   191→    if result.returncode != 0:
   192→        raise RuntimeError(f"ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
   193→
   194→    return float(result.stdout.strip())
   195→
```

```
1→"""Camera system: resolve keyframes from screenplay + timing → ffmpeg filter expressions.
     2→
     3→Camera coordinates:
     4→- x, y: normalized 0.0–1.0 (viewport center position)
     5→- zoom: multiplier (1.0 = full frame, 2.0 = native pixels)
     6→
     7→Pipeline:
     8→1. Resolve camera directives against timing manifest → keyframe list
     9→2. Generate ffmpeg crop+scale filter with interpolated expressions
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import math
    15→from dataclasses import dataclass
    16→from typing import List, Optional
    17→
    18→from demo.schema import CameraDirective, ConversationScene, ActionScene, Screenplay
    19→from demo.scenes import TimingManifest
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass
    23→class CameraKeyframe:
    24→    """Resolved camera keyframe with absolute timestamp."""
    25→    t: float       # Absolute time in seconds
    26→    zoom: float    # 1.0 = full frame
    27→    x: float       # 0.0–1.0
    28→    y: float       # 0.0–1.0
    29→    duration: float  # Transition time to reach this state
    30→    ease: str      # "linear", "ease-in", "ease-out", "ease-in-out"
    31→
    32→
    33→def resolve_keyframes(
    34→    screenplay: Screenplay,
    35→    manifest: TimingManifest,
    36→) -> List[CameraKeyframe]:
    37→    """Resolve camera directives from screenplay scenes against timing manifest.
    38→
    39→    Returns a sorted list of CameraKeyframes with absolute timestamps.
    40→    """
    41→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe] = []
    42→    scenes = screenplay.parsed_scenes()
    43→
    44→    for i, scene in enumerate(scenes):
    45→        if i >= len(manifest.scenes):
    46→            break
    47→        timing = manifest.scenes[i]
    48→
    49→        if isinstance(scene, ConversationScene):
    50→            if scene.camera:
    51→                t = _resolve_marker_time(scene.camera.at, timing)
    52→                if t is not None:
    53→                    keyframes.append(CameraKeyframe(
    54→                        t=t, zoom=scene.camera.zoom,
    55→                        x=scene.camera.x, y=scene.camera.y,
    56→                        duration=scene.camera.duration, ease=scene.camera.ease,
    57→                    ))
    58→            if scene.camera_response:
    59→                t = _resolve_marker_time(scene.camera_response.at, timing)
    60→                if t is None:
    61→                    t = timing.markers.get("response_start", timing.end_t)
    62→                if t is not None:
    63→                    keyframes.append(CameraKeyframe(
    64→                        t=t, zoom=scene.camera_response.zoom,
    65→                        x=scene.camera_response.x, y=scene.camera_response.y,
    66→                        duration=scene.camera_response.duration,
    67→                        ease=scene.camera_response.ease,
    68→                    ))
    69→
    70→        elif isinstance(scene, ActionScene) and scene.action == "camera":
    71→            t = timing.markers.get("camera", timing.start_t)
    72→            keyframes.append(CameraKeyframe(
    73→                t=t,
    74→                zoom=timing.markers.get("_zoom", 1.0),
    75→                x=timing.markers.get("_x", 0.5),
    76→                y=timing.markers.get("_y", 0.5),
    77→                duration=timing.markers.get("_duration", 0.5),
    78→                ease=scene.ease or "ease-in-out",
    79→            ))
    80→
    81→    keyframes.sort(key=lambda k: k.t)
    82→
    83→    # Ensure there's always a starting keyframe at t=0
    84→    if not keyframes or keyframes[0].t > 0.01:
    85→        keyframes.insert(0, CameraKeyframe(
    86→            t=0.0, zoom=1.0, x=0.5, y=0.5, duration=0.0, ease="linear",
    87→        ))
    88→
    89→    return keyframes
    90→
    91→
    92→def _resolve_marker_time(marker: str, timing) -> Optional[float]:
    93→    """Resolve a marker name to an absolute time from scene timing."""
    94→    if marker == "scene_start":
    95→        return timing.start_t
    96→    if marker == "scene_end":
    97→        return timing.end_t
    98→    return timing.markers.get(marker, timing.start_t)
    99→
   100→
   101→# ── Easing functions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   102→
   103→def _ease(t: float, ease_type: str) -> float:
   104→    """Apply easing to normalized t (0.0–1.0)."""
   105→    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, t))
   106→    if ease_type == "linear":
   107→        return t
   108→    elif ease_type == "ease-in":
   109→        return t * t
   110→    elif ease_type == "ease-out":
   111→        return 1.0 - (1.0 - t) * (1.0 - t)
   112→    elif ease_type == "ease-in-out":
   113→        # Smoothstep
   114→        return t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)
   115→    return t
   116→
   117→
   118→def _interpolate_keyframes(
   119→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe], t: float
   120→) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
   121→    """Interpolate zoom, x, y at time t given sorted keyframes."""
   122→    if not keyframes:
   123→        return 1.0, 0.5, 0.5
   124→
   125→    # Before first keyframe
   126→    if t <= keyframes[0].t:
   127→        kf = keyframes[0]
   128→        return kf.zoom, kf.x, kf.y
   129→
   130→    # Find the active transition
   131→    for i in range(1, len(keyframes)):
   132→        kf = keyframes[i]
   133→        prev = keyframes[i - 1]
   134→        transition_start = kf.t - kf.duration
   135→        if t <= kf.t:
   136→            if t < transition_start:
   137→                return prev.zoom, prev.x, prev.y
   138→            # In transition
   139→            progress = (t - transition_start) / kf.duration if kf.duration > 0 else 1.0
   140→            eased = _ease(progress, kf.ease)
   141→            zoom = prev.zoom + (kf.zoom - prev.zoom) * eased
   142→            x = prev.x + (kf.x - prev.x) * eased
   143→            y = prev.y + (kf.y - prev.y) * eased
   144→            return zoom, x, y
   145→
   146→    # After last keyframe
   147→    last = keyframes[-1]
   148→    return last.zoom, last.x, last.y
   149→
   150→
   151→# ── ffmpeg filter generation ───────────────────────────────────────────
   152→
   153→def generate_ffmpeg_filter(
   154→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe],
   155→    input_w: int,
   156→    input_h: int,
   157→    output_w: int,
   158→    output_h: int,
   159→    total_duration: float,
   160→    fps: int = 30,
   161→) -> str:
   162→    """Generate an ffmpeg crop+scale filter expression for camera keyframes.
   163→
   164→    Produces frame-by-frame crop parameters using ffmpeg expression syntax
   165→    with if(between(t,...)) chains for each transition segment.
   166→    """
   167→    if not keyframes or (len(keyframes) == 1 and keyframes[0].zoom == 1.0):
   168→        # No camera movement — just scale
   169→        return f"scale={output_w}:{output_h}"
   170→
   171→    # Build crop expressions for w, h, x, y as ffmpeg expression strings
   172→    w_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "w", total_duration)
   173→    h_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "h", total_duration)
   174→    x_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "x", total_duration)
   175→    y_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "y", total_duration)
   176→
   177→    return f"crop=w='{w_expr}':h='{h_expr}':x='{x_expr}':y='{y_expr}',scale={output_w}:{output_h}"
   178→
   179→
   180→def _build_expr(
   181→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe],
   182→    input_w: int,
   183→    input_h: int,
   184→    component: str,  # "w", "h", "x", "y"
   185→    total_duration: float,
   186→) -> str:
   187→    """Build an ffmpeg expression string for one crop component.
   188→
   189→    Uses nested if(between(t,start,end), interpolated_val, ...) chains.
   190→    """
   191→    aspect = input_w / input_h
   192→
   193→    def _val(zoom: float, cx: float, cy: float) -> float:
   194→        crop_w = input_w / zoom
   195→        crop_h = input_h / zoom
   196→        crop_x = cx * input_w - crop_w / 2
   197→        crop_y = cy * input_h - crop_h / 2
   198→        # Clamp
   199→        crop_x = max(0, min(crop_x, input_w - crop_w))
   200→        crop_y = max(0, min(crop_y, input_h - crop_h))
   201→        if component == "w":
   202→            return crop_w
   203→        elif component == "h":
   204→            return crop_h
   205→        elif component == "x":
   206→            return crop_x
   207→        elif component == "y":
   208→            return crop_y
   209→        return 0
   210→
   211→    if len(keyframes) == 1:
   212→        kf = keyframes[0]
   213→        return str(int(_val(kf.zoom, kf.x, kf.y)))
   214→
   215→    # Sample the interpolation function at regular intervals and build a
   216→    # piecewise-linear ffmpeg expression. This avoids complex nested
   217→    # if/between logic and correctly handles holds between transitions.
   218→    sample_rate = 10  # samples per second
   219→    n_samples = max(2, int(total_duration * sample_rate) + 1)
   220→    dt = total_duration / (n_samples - 1)
   221→
   222→    samples = []
   223→    for i in range(n_samples):
   224→        t = i * dt
   225→        zoom, cx, cy = _interpolate_keyframes(keyframes, t)
   226→        val = int(round(_val(zoom, cx, cy)))
   227→        samples.append((t, val))
   228→
   229→    # Deduplicate: only keep samples where value changes (run-length encode)
   230→    compressed = [samples[0]]
   231→    for i in range(1, len(samples)):
   232→        if samples[i][1] != samples[i - 1][1]:
   233→            # Ensure we keep the last sample of the previous constant run
   234→            if compressed[-1] != samples[i - 1]:
   235→                compressed.append(samples[i - 1])
   236→            compressed.append(samples[i])
   237→    # Always include the final sample
   238→    if compressed[-1] != samples[-1]:
   239→        compressed.append(samples[-1])
   240→
   241→    # If all samples are the same, return a constant
   242→    if all(s[1] == compressed[0][1] for s in compressed):
   243→        return str(compressed[0][1])
   244→
   245→    # Build ffmpeg expression: chain of if(between(t,start,end), lerp, ...)
   246→    # Work backwards so the else branch is always the next segment
   247→    last_val = compressed[-1][1]
   248→    expr = str(last_val)
   249→
   250→    for i in range(len(compressed) - 2, -1, -1):
   251→        t0, v0 = compressed[i]
   252→        t1, v1 = compressed[i + 1]
   253→        if v0 == v1:
   254→            # Constant segment — hold value
   255→            expr = f"if(between(t,{t0:.3f},{t1:.3f}),{v0},{expr})"
   256→        else:
   257→            # Linear interpolation between samples
   258→            expr = (
   259→                f"if(between(t,{t0:.3f},{t1:.3f}),"
   260→                f"{v0}+({v1}-{v0})*(t-{t0:.3f})/{t1 - t0:.3f}"
   261→                f",{expr})"
   262→            )
   263→
   264→    return expr
   265→
```

```
1→"""Scene registry, PlaybackContext, and base protocol."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import sys
     7→import time
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Protocol
    11→
    12→from rich.console import Console
    13→
    14→from hermes_cli.skin_engine import SkinConfig, load_skin
    15→
    16→
    17→# ── Timing manifest ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class TimingMarker:
    21→    """A single named timestamp in the recording."""
    22→    name: str
    23→    t: float
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass
    27→class SceneTiming:
    28→    """Timing data for one scene."""
    29→    index: int
    30→    scene_type: str  # "conversation" | "action"
    31→    action: Optional[str] = None
    32→    start_t: float = 0.0
    33→    end_t: float = 0.0
    34→    markers: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass
    38→class TimingManifest:
    39→    """Full timing data for a recording session."""
    40→    scenes: List[SceneTiming] = field(default_factory=list)
    41→    total_duration: float = 0.0
    42→
    43→    def add_scene(self, scene: SceneTiming):
    44→        self.scenes.append(scene)
    45→
    46→    def finalize(self):
    47→        if self.scenes:
    48→            self.total_duration = max(s.end_t for s in self.scenes)
    49→
    50→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    51→        result = {"scenes": [], "total_duration": self.total_duration}
    52→        for s in self.scenes:
    53→            entry: Dict[str, Any] = {
    54→                "index": s.index,
    55→                "type": s.scene_type,
    56→                "start_t": round(s.start_t, 3),
    57→                "end_t": round(s.end_t, 3),
    58→            }
    59→            if s.action:
    60→                entry["action"] = s.action
    61→            if s.markers:
    62→                entry["markers"] = {k: round(v, 3) for k, v in s.markers.items()}
    63→            result["scenes"].append(entry)
    64→        return result
    65→
    66→    def save(self, path: Path):
    67→        path.write_text(json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=2))
    68→
    69→
    70→# ── ANSI helpers (skin-aware) ───────────────────────────────────────────
    71→
    72→def hex_to_ansi(hex_color: str) -> str:
    73→    """Convert #RRGGBB to ANSI 24-bit foreground escape."""
    74→    h = hex_color.lstrip("#")
    75→    if len(h) != 6:
    76→        return ""
    77→    r, g, b = int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16)
    78→    return f"\033[38;2;{r};{g};{b}m"
    79→
    80→
    81→_BOLD = "\033[1m"
    82→_DIM = "\033[2m"
    83→_RST = "\033[0m"
    84→
    85→
    86→# ── Playback context ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    87→
    88→@dataclass
    89→class PlaybackContext:
    90→    """Shared state passed to every scene handler during playback."""
    91→
    92→    skin: SkinConfig
    93→    console: Console
    94→    typing_speed: float = 0.04
    95→    pause_between: float = 1.0
    96→    scene_index: int = 0
    97→    recording_start: float = 0.0  # monotonic time of recording start
    98→    timing: Optional[TimingManifest] = None
    99→
   100→    # ── skin ANSI shortcuts ──────────────────────────────────────────
   101→
   102→    @property
   103→    def ansi_prompt(self) -> str:
   104→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("prompt", "#FFF8DC"))
   105→
   106→    @property
   107→    def ansi_banner_title(self) -> str:
   108→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_title", "#FFD700"))
   109→
   110→    @property
   111→    def ansi_banner_border(self) -> str:
   112→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32"))
   113→
   114→    @property
   115→    def ansi_banner_accent(self) -> str:
   116→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00"))
   117→
   118→    @property
   119→    def ansi_banner_dim(self) -> str:
   120→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B"))
   121→
   122→    @property
   123→    def ansi_banner_text(self) -> str:
   124→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_text", "#FFF8DC"))
   125→
   126→    # ── timing helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   127→
   128→    def now(self) -> float:
   129→        """Seconds since recording started."""
   130→        return time.monotonic() - self.recording_start
   131→
   132→    def mark(self, scene_timing: SceneTiming, name: str):
   133→        """Record a named timing marker on the current scene."""
   134→        scene_timing.markers[name] = self.now()
   135→
   136→
   137→# ── Scene handler protocol ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   138→
   139→class SceneHandler(Protocol):
   140→    """Protocol for scene handler functions."""
   141→
   142→    def __call__(self, scene: Any, ctx: PlaybackContext) -> None: ...
   143→
   144→
   145→# ── Scene registry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   146→
   147→_registry: Dict[str, SceneHandler] = {}
   148→
   149→
   150→def register(scene_type: str) -> Callable:
   151→    """Decorator to register a scene handler."""
   152→    def decorator(fn: SceneHandler) -> SceneHandler:
   153→        _registry[scene_type] = fn
   154→        return fn
   155→    return decorator
   156→
   157→
   158→def get_handler(scene_type: str) -> Optional[SceneHandler]:
   159→    """Look up a registered scene handler."""
   160→    return _registry.get(scene_type)
   161→
   162→
   163→def dispatch(scene: Any, ctx: PlaybackContext) -> None:
   164→    """Dispatch a parsed scene to its handler."""
   165→    from demo.schema import ActionScene, ConversationScene
   166→
   167→    if isinstance(scene, ActionScene):
   168→        handler = get_handler(f"action:{scene.action}")
   169→        if handler is None:
   170→            handler = get_handler("action")
   171→        if handler:
   172→            handler(scene, ctx)
   173→    elif isinstance(scene, ConversationScene):
   174→        handler = get_handler("conversation")
   175→        if handler:
   176→            handler(scene, ctx)
   177→
```

```
1→"""Conversation scene: user prompt -> spinner -> tools -> response."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import random
     6→import sys
     7→import threading
     8→import time
     9→
    10→from rich.markdown import Markdown
    11→from rich.panel import Panel
    12→from rich import box as rich_box
    13→
    14→from demo.schema import ConversationScene
    15→from demo.scenes import (
    16→    PlaybackContext, SceneTiming, _BOLD, _DIM, _RST,
    17→    hex_to_ansi, register,
    18→)
    19→
    20→# ── Spinner frames ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    21→
    22→SPINNER_FRAMES = ["\u280b", "\u2819", "\u2839", "\u2838", "\u283c", "\u2834", "\u2826", "\u2827", "\u2807", "\u280f"]
    23→
    24→# Defaults (used when skin has none)
    25→_DEFAULT_THINKING_FACES = [
    26→    "(｡•́︿•̀｡)", "(◔_◔)", "(¬‿¬)", "( •_•)>⌐■-■", "(⌐■_■)",
    27→    "(´･_･`)", "◉_◉", "(°ロ°)", "( ˘⌣˘)♡", "ヽ(>∀<☆)☆",
    28→    "٩(๑❛ᴗ❛๑)۶", "(⊙_⊙)", "(¬_¬)", "( ͡° ͜ʖ ͡°)", "ಠ_ಠ",
    29→]
    30→
    31→_DEFAULT_THINKING_VERBS = [
    32→    "pondering", "contemplating", "musing", "cogitating", "ruminating",
    33→    "deliberating", "mulling", "reflecting", "processing", "reasoning",
    34→    "analyzing", "computing", "synthesizing", "formulating", "brainstorming",
    35→]
    36→
    37→
    38→def _get_faces(ctx: PlaybackContext) -> list:
    39→    faces = ctx.skin.get_spinner_list("thinking_faces")
    40→    return faces if faces else _DEFAULT_THINKING_FACES
    41→
    42→
    43→def _get_verbs(ctx: PlaybackContext) -> list:
    44→    verbs = ctx.skin.get_spinner_list("thinking_verbs")
    45→    return verbs if verbs else _DEFAULT_THINKING_VERBS
    46→
    47→
    48→# ── Typing simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    49→
    50→def type_text(text: str, speed: float, color: str):
    51→    """Simulate typing character by character."""
    52→    for ch in text:
    53→        sys.stdout.write(f"{color}{ch}{_RST}")
    54→        sys.stdout.flush()
    55→        if ch in " \t":
    56→            time.sleep(speed * 0.5)
    57→        elif ch in ".,!?;:":
    58→            time.sleep(speed * 2.5)
    59→        else:
    60→            time.sleep(speed * (0.7 + random.random() * 0.6))
    61→
    62→
    63→# ── Demo spinner ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    64→
    65→class DemoSpinner:
    66→    """Animated braille spinner with kawaii faces."""
    67→
    68→    def __init__(self, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    69→        self.ctx = ctx
    70→        self.running = False
    71→        self.thread = None
    72→        self.start_time = 0.0
    73→        self.frame_idx = 0
    74→        self.message = ""
    75→        self.last_len = 0
    76→        self._wings = ctx.skin.get_spinner_wings()
    77→
    78→    def start(self, message: str = ""):
    79→        self.message = message
    80→        self.running = True
    81→        self.start_time = time.time()
    82→        self.frame_idx = 0
    83→        self.last_len = 0
    84→        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._animate, daemon=True)
    85→        self.thread.start()
    86→
    87→    def _animate(self):
    88→        while self.running:
    89→            frame = SPINNER_FRAMES[self.frame_idx % len(SPINNER_FRAMES)]
    90→            elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time
    91→            if self._wings:
    92→                left, right = self._wings[self.frame_idx % len(self._wings)]
    93→                line = f"  {left} {frame} {self.message} {right} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
    94→            else:
    95→                line = f"  {frame} {self.message} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
    96→            pad = max(self.last_len - len(line), 0)
    97→            sys.stdout.write(f"\r{line}{' ' * pad}")
    98→            sys.stdout.flush()
    99→            self.last_len = len(line)
   100→            self.frame_idx += 1
   101→            time.sleep(0.12)
   102→
   103→    def update(self, message: str):
   104→        self.message = message
   105→
   106→    def stop(self):
   107→        self.running = False
   108→        if self.thread:
   109→            self.thread.join(timeout=0.5)
   110→        blanks = " " * max(self.last_len + 5, 40)
   111→        sys.stdout.write(f"\r{blanks}\r")
   112→        sys.stdout.flush()
   113→
   114→
   115→# ── Tool progress lines ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   116→
   117→def show_tool_line(tool: dict, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   118→    """Print a tool progress line."""
   119→    icon = tool.get("icon", "\u26a1")
   120→    verb = tool.get("verb", "tool")
   121→    detail = tool.get("detail", "")
   122→    duration = tool.get("duration", "0.5s")
   123→    prefix = ctx.skin.tool_prefix
   124→
   125→    line = f"  {prefix} {icon} {verb:9} {detail}  {duration}"
   126→    sys.stdout.write(f"{_DIM}{line}{_RST}\n")
   127→    sys.stdout.flush()
   128→
   129→
   130→# ── Response rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   131→
   132→def show_response(text: str, label: str, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   133→    """Show agent response in a Rich panel using skin colors."""
   134→    border_color = ctx.skin.get_color("response_border", "#FFD700")
   135→    ctx.console.print(Panel(
   136→        Markdown(text),
   137→        title=f"[bold]{label}[/bold]",
   138→        title_align="left",
   139→        border_style=border_color,
   140→        box=rich_box.HORIZONTALS,
   141→        padding=(1, 2),
   142→    ))
   143→
   144→
   145→# ── Scene handler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   146→
   147→@register("conversation")
   148→def play_conversation(scene: ConversationScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   149→    """Play a conversation scene: user → spinner → tools → response."""
   150→    typing_speed = scene.typing_speed or ctx.typing_speed
   151→    prompt_color = ctx.ansi_prompt
   152→    prompt_symbol = ctx.skin.get_branding("prompt_symbol", "\u276f ")
   153→    response_label = scene.response_label or ctx.skin.get_branding("response_label", " \u2695 Hermes ")
   154→
   155→    # Timing
   156→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
   157→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="conversation"
   158→    )
   159→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
   160→
   161→    # --- User message ---
   162→    if scene.user:
   163→        time.sleep(scene.pre_pause)
   164→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "user_start")
   165→
   166→        # Show prompt
   167→        sys.stdout.write(f"\n{prompt_color}{prompt_symbol}{_RST}")
   168→        sys.stdout.flush()
   169→        time.sleep(0.3)
   170→
   171→        # Type the message
   172→        type_text(scene.user, speed=typing_speed, color=ctx.ansi_banner_text)
   173→        time.sleep(0.6)
   174→
   175→        # "Submit" — show as gold bullet
   176→        gold = hex_to_ansi(ctx.skin.get_color("banner_title", "#FFD700"))
   177→        sys.stdout.write(f"\r\033[K")
   178→        sys.stdout.write(f"{gold}\u25cf {_BOLD}{scene.user}{_RST}\n")
   179→        sys.stdout.flush()
   180→        time.sleep(0.3)
   181→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "user_end")
   182→
   183→    # --- Spinner (thinking) ---
   184→    ctx.mark(scene_timing, "thinking_start")
   185→    spinner = DemoSpinner(ctx)
   186→    face = random.choice(_get_faces(ctx))
   187→    verb = random.choice(_get_verbs(ctx))
   188→    spinner.start(f"{face} {verb}...")
   189→
   190→    thinking_time = scene.thinking_time
   191→    if thinking_time > 2.0:
   192→        time.sleep(thinking_time * 0.4)
   193→        face2 = random.choice(_get_faces(ctx))
   194→        verb2 = random.choice(_get_verbs(ctx))
   195→        spinner.update(f"{face2} {verb2}...")
   196→        time.sleep(thinking_time * 0.6)
   197→    else:
   198→        time.sleep(thinking_time)
   199→
   200→    spinner.stop()
   201→    ctx.mark(scene_timing, "thinking_end")
   202→
   203→    # --- Tool progress lines ---
   204→    if scene.tools:
   205→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "tools_start")
   206→        for tool in scene.tools:
   207→            tool_dict = tool.model_dump() if hasattr(tool, "model_dump") else tool
   208→            show_tool_line(tool_dict, ctx)
   209→            tool_delay = tool_dict.get("delay", 0.15)
   210→            time.sleep(tool_delay)
   211→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "tools_end")
   212→
   213→    # --- Response ---
   214→    if scene.response:
   215→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "response_start")
   216→        show_response(scene.response, label=response_label, ctx=ctx)
   217→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "response_end")
   218→
   219→    time.sleep(scene.post_pause)
   220→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
   221→
   222→    if ctx.timing:
   223→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
   224→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import os

    10→
    11→from demo.schema import ActionScene
    12→from demo.scenes import (

    19→# ── Generic action dispatcher ──────────────────────────────────────────
    20→
    21→@register("action")
    22→def play_action(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    23→    """Dispatch an action scene to the right sub-handler."""
    24→    handlers = {
    25→        "pause": _action_pause,
    26→        "clear": _action_clear,
    27→        "print": _action_print,
    28→        "banner": _action_banner,
    29→        "type_command": _action_type_command,
    30→        "camera": _action_camera,
    31→    }
    32→    handler = handlers.get(scene.action)
    33→    if handler:

    36→
    37→# ── Individual action handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────
    38→
    39→def _action_pause(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    40→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    41→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="pause"
    42→    )
    43→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    44→    time.sleep(scene.duration or 1.0)
    45→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
    46→    if ctx.timing:
    47→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
    48→
    49→
    50→def _action_clear(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    51→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    52→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="clear"
    53→    )
    54→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    55→    os.system("clear")
    56→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
    57→    if ctx.timing:
    58→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _action_print(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    62→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    63→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="print"
    64→    )
    65→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    66→    text = scene.text or ""
    67→    if scene.color:
    68→        ctx.console.print(f"[{scene.color}]{text}[/]")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(text)
    71→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
    72→    if ctx.timing:
    73→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
    74→
    75→
    76→def _action_type_command(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    77→    """Type a raw shell command (not the agent prompt)."""
    78→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    79→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="type_command"
    80→    )
    81→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    82→
    83→    cmd = scene.command or ""

    85→
    86→    sys.stdout.write(f"\n{_DIM}{prefix}{_RST}")
    87→    sys.stdout.flush()
    88→    time.sleep(0.3)
    89→    type_text(cmd, speed=ctx.typing_speed, color=f"{_BOLD}")
    90→    time.sleep(0.5)
    91→    sys.stdout.write("\n")
    92→    sys.stdout.flush()
    93→
    94→    output = scene.output or ""
    95→    if output:
    96→        for line in output.split("\n"):
    97→            sys.stdout.write(f"{_DIM}{line}{_RST}\n")
    98→            sys.stdout.flush()
    99→            time.sleep(0.05)

   106→def _action_camera(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):

   199→
   200→# ── Default banner art (Hermes caduceus + logo) ────────────────────────
   201→
   202→def _default_caduceus(c1: str, c2: str, c3: str, c4: str) -> str:
   203→    """Default Hermes caduceus art with 4-color gradient.
   204→
   205→    Colors map: c1=border, c2=accent, c3=title, c4=dim
   206→    Original uses: #CD7F32, #FFBF00, #FFD700, #B8860B
   207→    """
   208→    return (
   209→        f"[{c1}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣀⡀⠀⣀⣀⠀⢀⣀⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
```

    82→              resp.raise_for_status()

Perfect! I now have a comprehensive understanding of the demo architecture. Let me provide you with a complete summary.

## Complete Demo Architecture & Data Flow

### Overview
The demo system is a **screenplay-based terminal session recorder** that converts YAML scripts into polished MP4 videos with optional camera effects. It simulates a conversational AI agent (Hermes) executing tasks in real-time with visual effects, tool calls, and responses.

---

## Core Architecture

### 1. **Entry Point: `__main__.py`**
- CLI parser supporting three modes:
  - `--play`: Terminal preview (real-time playback)
  - `-o output.mp4`: Full pipeline (record → render → camera → encode)
  - `--skin`: Theme override
  - `--typing-speed`: Speed control
  - `--dry-run`: Print pipeline commands without executing
- Delegates to `player.py` (--play) or `pipeline.py` (full video)

---

### 2. **Data Model: `schema.py`**
Pydantic models defining the screenplay structure:

**Scene Types:**
- **ConversationScene**: User message → thinking spinner → tool calls → response
  - `user`: User query text
  - `tools`: List of `ToolEntry` (icon, verb, detail, duration, delay)
  - `response`: Agent response (markdown)
  - `thinking_time`: Spinner duration
  - `typing_speed`: Char speed override
  - `camera`: Zoom/pan directives for thinking/tools phase
  - `camera_response`: Zoom/pan directives for response phase

- **ActionScene**: Non-conversation actions
  - `pause`: Delay (seconds)
  - `clear`: Clear terminal
  - `print`: Print text
  - `banner`: Show Hermes welcome banner
  - `type_command`: Simulate typing a shell command
  - `camera`: Standalone camera keyframe

**Camera Directive:**
```python
class CameraDirective(BaseModel):
    zoom: float = 1.0        # 1.0 = full frame, 2.0 = 2x zoom
    x: float = 0.5           # Horizontal position (0.0–1.0)
    y: float = 0.5           # Vertical position (0.0–1.0)
    at: str = "scene_start"  # Timing marker: scene_start, scene_end, etc.
    duration: float = 0.5    # Transition time (seconds)
    ease: str = "ease-in-out" # Easing function
```

**Output Config:**
- `width: 2560, height: 1440`: Render resolution (2x for quality)
- `final_width: 1280, final_height: 720`: Output resolution
- `fps: 30`
- `font_size, font_family`: Rendering parameters

---

### 3. **Terminal Playback: `player.py`**
Plays screenplay in real-time for `--play` mode:

**Key Functions:**
- `load_screenplay()`: Parses YAML, validates with Pydantic, stores source path
- `play()`: Executes scenes in order
  - Creates `PlaybackContext` (shared state)
  - Dispatches each scene to registered handler
  - Records timing manifest (JSON) if `--timing` specified
  - Emits ANSI-colored terminal output

**PlaybackContext:**
- `skin`: Theme configuration
- `console`: Rich console for rendering
- `typing_speed`: Base char delay
- `recording_start`: Monotonic time baseline
- `timing`: TimingManifest for recording markers
- Helper methods: `now()` (elapsed time), `mark()` (record event time)

---

### 4. **Scene Rendering: `scenes/`**

**Registry System (`__init__.py`):**
- Central dispatch via `dispatch(scene, ctx)`
- Scene handlers registered with `@register()` decorator
- Supports type-specific handlers (e.g., `action:camera`)

**Timing Manifest (`__init__.py`):**
```python
@dataclass
class SceneTiming:
    index: int
    scene_type: str  # "conversation" | "action"
    action: Optional[str]  # "pause", "clear", "banner", etc.
    start_t: float   # Absolute time (seconds since record start)
    end_t: float
    markers: Dict[str, float]  # Named events: user_start, thinking_start, etc.
```
- Saved as JSON after playback for camera post-processing

**Conversation Handler (`conversation.py`):**
1. **Pre-pause**: Wait before user input
2. **User prompt**: Show `$ ` and type message character-by-character
3. **Thinking spinner**: Braille animation with random kawaii faces/verbs
   - Faces from skin or defaults: `(｡•́︿•̀｡)`, `(◔_◔)`, etc.
   - Verbs: pondering, contemplating, cogitating, etc.
4. **Tool progress**: Display tool calls in dim text
5. **Response**: Rich markdown panel with border colors from skin
6. **Post-pause**: Wait after response

Typing simulation:
- Space/tab: 0.5x speed
- Punctuation: 2.5x speed
- Letters: 0.7–1.3x speed (randomized for naturalness)

**Action Handler (`action.py`):**
- `_action_pause()`: Wait
- `_action_clear()`: Clear terminal
- `_action_print()`: Print colored text
- `_action_banner()`: Show welcome screen with Hermes caduceus art + logo
- `_action_type_command()`: Type shell command and simulate output
- `_action_camera()`: Record camera keyframe (no visual, timing only)

---

### 5. **Video Pipeline: `pipeline.py`**

**Flow (4 Steps):**

```
[Screenplay] → [1. asciinema record] → [.cast file]
                                          ↓
                       [2. agg render] → [.gif file]
                                          ↓
           [Timing manifest] → [3. Camera resolve] → [FFmpeg filter chain]
                                                      ↓
                                    [4. ffmpeg encode] → [.mp4]
```

**Step 1: Record with asciinema**
- Runs player in `--play` mode under asciinema
- Records all terminal output + timing
- Produces `.cast` file (JSON format: timed sequences of terminal events)
- Terminal size: 200 cols × 50 rows (configurable)
- Environment: `TERM=xterm-256color`

**Step 2: Render with agg**
- Converts `.cast` → `.gif` with rasterized text
- Applies font rendering at specified size/family
- Outputs at native resolution (2560×1440)
- Why GIF? agg's native output; ffmpeg converts to MP4

**Step 3: Resolve camera keyframes**
- Loads timing manifest (JSON with scene markers)
- Iterates screenplay scenes, matching camera directives
- Resolves marker names to absolute timestamps:
  - `"at: scene_start"` → timing.start_t
  - `"at: response_start"` → timing.markers["response_start"]
- Sorts keyframes by time
- Injects default `t=0` keyframe if missing
- Scales keyframe times if GIF duration differs from manifest
  - Accounts for asciinema startup overhead + agg timing adjustments

**Step 4: Encode with ffmpeg**
- Applies camera filter (crop + scale) if keyframes exist
- Otherwise just scales to final resolution
- Uses libx264 codec, preset=slow, crf=18 (high quality)
- Output: 1280×720 @ 30fps

---

### 6. **Camera System: `camera.py`**

**Keyframe Resolution:**
- Matches screenplay camera directives against timing manifest
- Produces list of `CameraKeyframe`:
  ```python
  @dataclass
  class CameraKeyframe:
      t: float       # Absolute time
      zoom: float    # 1.0 = full, 2.0 = 2x
      x, y: float    # Viewport center (0.0–1.0)
      duration: float  # Transition time
      ease: str      # linear, ease-in, ease-out, ease-in-out
  ```

**Easing Functions:**
- `linear`: No easing
- `ease-in`: t²
- `ease-out`: 1 - (1-t)²
- `ease-in-out`: Smoothstep (t² × (3 - 2t))

**Interpolation:**
- Between keyframes, interpolates zoom/x/y smoothly
- Applies easing function to progress (0.0–1.0)
- Holds previous values before transition starts

**FFmpeg Filter Generation:**
- Builds dynamic crop+scale filter expression
- Samples interpolation at 10 samples/sec
- Generates piecewise-linear ffmpeg expression using `if(between(t,...), ...)`
- Crop math:
  ```
  crop_w = input_w / zoom
  crop_h = input_h / zoom
  crop_x = center_x * input_w - crop_w / 2
  crop_y = center_y * input_h - crop_h / 2
  ```
- Final filter: `crop=w='expr_w':h='expr_h':x='expr_x':y='expr_y',scale=1280:720`

---

### 7. **Tool Wrappers: `recorder.py`**

**External Tools Required:**
1. **asciinema** (`pip install asciinema`)
   - Records terminal I/O streams with timing
   - Produces `.cast` file (ASCII cast v2 JSON format)

2. **agg** (`cargo install agg` or `brew install agg`)
   - Renders `.cast` → `.gif` with font rasterization
   - Supports configurable fonts, sizes, FPS
   - Uses GPU acceleration (Skia)

3. **ffmpeg** (`brew install ffmpeg`)
   - Video encoding, filtering
   - FFmpeg expressions for dynamic crop/zoom
   - Converts GIF → MP4 with h.264 codec

4. **ffprobe** (bundled with ffmpeg)
   - Extracts video dimensions and duration
   - Used for timing synchronization

---

## YAML Script Structure

All scripts define a `Screenplay` with:
- `title`: Display name
- `skin`: Theme (e.g., "default", "ares")
- `output`: Rendering config
- `typing_speed`: Base char delay (seconds)
- `pause_between`: Default inter-scene pause
- `scenes`: List of conversation or action scenes

### Example: `example_with_camera.yaml`
```yaml
title: "Hermes Agent Demo — Camera Example"
output:
  width: 2560
  height: 1440
  final_width: 1280
  final_height: 720
  fps: 30

scenes:
  - user: "Search for the latest AI agent frameworks..."
    thinking_time: 3.0
    camera:                    # Zoom into prompt area
      zoom: 1.8
      y: 0.15
      at: "user_start"
      duration: 0.8
      ease: "ease-in-out"
    tools:
      - icon: "🔍"
        verb: "search"
        detail: '"AI agent frameworks..."'
    camera_response:          # Pan to response area
      zoom: 1.4
      y: 0.65
      at: "response_start"
    response: "Here's a comparison..."
  
  - action: camera            # Reset zoom
    zoom: 1.0
    duration: 1.0
```

---

## Complete Data Flow Example

1. **User runs:** `python -m demo scripts/example_with_camera.yaml -o demo.mp4`

2. **`__main__.py`** parses args, loads screenplay via `player.py`

3. **`pipeline.py` executes:**
   
   a) **Record** (asciinema):
   ```bash
   asciinema rec --cols=200 --rows=50 \
     --command 'python -m demo --play --timing timing.json scripts/example_with_camera.yaml' \
     output.cast
   ```
   - Player runs in terminal, emits ANSI, timing markers
   
   b) **Render** (agg):
   ```bash
   agg --font-size=22 --font-family=Menlo --fps-cap=30 \
     output.cast output.gif
   ```
   - 2560×1440 rasterized output
   
   c) **Resolve camera**:
   - Load `timing.json` with scene timings
   - For scene 0 (conversation): extract `camera.at="user_start"` marker
   - Create keyframe at t=~0.8s, zoom=1.8, y=0.15, duration=0.8s
   - Create keyframe at t=~3.2s, zoom=1.4, y=0.65 (camera_response)
   - Build FFmpeg filter chain with interpolated crop/pan
   
   d) **Encode** (ffmpeg):
   ```bash
   ffmpeg -i output.gif -vf 'crop=w=\'if(between(t,0,0.8),1422,...), ...\':h=\'...\':x=\'...\':y=\'...\',scale=1280:720' \
     -c:v libx264 -preset slow -crf 18 -pix_fmt yuv420p -r 30 \
     demo.mp4
   ```
   - Final output: 1280×720 @ 30fps with smooth camera movements

4. **Result:** Professional demo video with synchronized zoom/pan effects tied to agent narration

---

## Key Design Insights

1. **Decoupled Pipeline**: Each stage (record → render → camera → encode) is independent, allowing partial reruns
2. **Timing Manifest**: Bridges real-time playback to post-processing; markers (user_start, thinking_start, etc.) enable precise camera sync
3. **Skinning System**: Integrates with `hermes_cli.skin_engine` for consistent theming across demos
4. **Natural Typing**: Randomized delays + punctuation pauses make terminal interaction feel organic
5. **Camera Flexibility**: 
   - Scene-level directives (during conversation)
   - Standalone action scenes (explicit keyframes)
   - Smooth easing transitions between states
6. **High-Quality Output**: Renders at 2x resolution, then scales down to reduce aliasing

  - `example_with_camera.yaml` (with zoom/pan)
  - `test_quick.yaml` (minimal, for testing)

```
1→"""Camera system: resolve keyframes from screenplay + timing → ffmpeg filter expressions.
     2→
     3→Camera coordinates:
     4→- x, y: normalized 0.0–1.0 (viewport center position)
     5→- zoom: multiplier (1.0 = full frame, 2.0 = native pixels)
     6→
     7→Pipeline:
     8→1. Resolve camera directives against timing manifest → keyframe list
     9→2. Generate ffmpeg crop+scale filter with interpolated expressions
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import math
    15→from dataclasses import dataclass
    16→from typing import List, Optional
    17→
    18→from demo.schema import CameraDirective, ConversationScene, ActionScene, Screenplay
    19→from demo.scenes import TimingManifest
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass
    23→class CameraKeyframe:
    24→    """Resolved camera keyframe with absolute timestamp."""
    25→    t: float       # Absolute time in seconds
    26→    zoom: float    # 1.0 = full frame
    27→    x: float       # 0.0–1.0
    28→    y: float       # 0.0–1.0
    29→    duration: float  # Transition time to reach this state
    30→    ease: str      # "linear", "ease-in", "ease-out", "ease-in-out"
    31→
    32→
    33→def resolve_keyframes(
    34→    screenplay: Screenplay,
    35→    manifest: TimingManifest,
    36→) -> List[CameraKeyframe]:
    37→    """Resolve camera directives from screenplay scenes against timing manifest.
    38→
    39→    Returns a sorted list of CameraKeyframes with absolute timestamps.
    40→    """
    41→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe] = []
    42→    scenes = screenplay.parsed_scenes()
    43→
    44→    for i, scene in enumerate(scenes):
    45→        if i >= len(manifest.scenes):
    46→            break
    47→        timing = manifest.scenes[i]
    48→
    49→        if isinstance(scene, ConversationScene):
    50→            if scene.camera:
    51→                t = _resolve_marker_time(scene.camera.at, timing)
    52→                if t is not None:
    53→                    keyframes.append(CameraKeyframe(
    54→                        t=t, zoom=scene.camera.zoom,
    55→                        x=scene.camera.x, y=scene.camera.y,
    56→                        duration=scene.camera.duration, ease=scene.camera.ease,
    57→                    ))
    58→            if scene.camera_response:
    59→                t = _resolve_marker_time(scene.camera_response.at, timing)
    60→                if t is None:
    61→                    t = timing.markers.get("response_start", timing.end_t)
    62→                if t is not None:
    63→                    keyframes.append(CameraKeyframe(
    64→                        t=t, zoom=scene.camera_response.zoom,
    65→                        x=scene.camera_response.x, y=scene.camera_response.y,
    66→                        duration=scene.camera_response.duration,
    67→                        ease=scene.camera_response.ease,
    68→                    ))
    69→
    70→        elif isinstance(scene, ActionScene) and scene.action == "camera":
    71→            t = timing.markers.get("camera", timing.start_t)
    72→            keyframes.append(CameraKeyframe(
    73→                t=t,
    74→                zoom=timing.markers.get("_zoom", 1.0),
    75→                x=timing.markers.get("_x", 0.5),
    76→                y=timing.markers.get("_y", 0.5),
    77→                duration=timing.markers.get("_duration", 0.5),
    78→                ease=scene.ease or "ease-in-out",
    79→            ))
    80→
    81→    keyframes.sort(key=lambda k: k.t)
    82→
    83→    # Ensure there's always a starting keyframe at t=0
    84→    if not keyframes or keyframes[0].t > 0.01:
    85→        keyframes.insert(0, CameraKeyframe(
    86→            t=0.0, zoom=1.0, x=0.5, y=0.5, duration=0.0, ease="linear",
    87→        ))
    88→
    89→    return keyframes
    90→
    91→
    92→def _resolve_marker_time(marker: str, timing) -> Optional[float]:
    93→    """Resolve a marker name to an absolute time from scene timing."""
    94→    if marker == "scene_start":
    95→        return timing.start_t
    96→    if marker == "scene_end":
    97→        return timing.end_t
    98→    return timing.markers.get(marker, timing.start_t)
    99→
   100→
   101→# ── Easing functions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   102→
   103→def _ease(t: float, ease_type: str) -> float:
   104→    """Apply easing to normalized t (0.0–1.0)."""
   105→    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, t))
   106→    if ease_type == "linear":
   107→        return t
   108→    elif ease_type == "ease-in":
   109→        return t * t
   110→    elif ease_type == "ease-out":
   111→        return 1.0 - (1.0 - t) * (1.0 - t)
   112→    elif ease_type == "ease-in-out":
   113→        # Smoothstep
   114→        return t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)
   115→    return t
   116→
   117→
   118→def _interpolate_keyframes(
   119→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe], t: float
   120→) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
   121→    """Interpolate zoom, x, y at time t given sorted keyframes."""
   122→    if not keyframes:
   123→        return 1.0, 0.5, 0.5
   124→
   125→    # Before first keyframe
   126→    if t <= keyframes[0].t:
   127→        kf = keyframes[0]
   128→        return kf.zoom, kf.x, kf.y
   129→
   130→    # Find the active transition
   131→    for i in range(1, len(keyframes)):
   132→        kf = keyframes[i]
   133→        prev = keyframes[i - 1]
   134→        transition_start = kf.t - kf.duration
   135→        if t <= kf.t:
   136→            if t < transition_start:
   137→                return prev.zoom, prev.x, prev.y
   138→            # In transition
   139→            progress = (t - transition_start) / kf.duration if kf.duration > 0 else 1.0
   140→            eased = _ease(progress, kf.ease)
   141→            zoom = prev.zoom + (kf.zoom - prev.zoom) * eased
   142→            x = prev.x + (kf.x - prev.x) * eased
   143→            y = prev.y + (kf.y - prev.y) * eased
   144→            return zoom, x, y
   145→
   146→    # After last keyframe
   147→    last = keyframes[-1]
   148→    return last.zoom, last.x, last.y
   149→
   150→
   151→# ── ffmpeg filter generation ───────────────────────────────────────────
   152→
   153→def generate_ffmpeg_filter(
   154→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe],
   155→    input_w: int,
   156→    input_h: int,
   157→    output_w: int,
   158→    output_h: int,
   159→    total_duration: float,
   160→    fps: int = 30,
   161→) -> str:
   162→    """Generate an ffmpeg crop+scale filter expression for camera keyframes.
   163→
   164→    Produces frame-by-frame crop parameters using ffmpeg expression syntax
   165→    with if(between(t,...)) chains for each transition segment.
   166→    """
   167→    if not keyframes or (len(keyframes) == 1 and keyframes[0].zoom == 1.0):
   168→        # No camera movement — just scale
   169→        return f"scale={output_w}:{output_h}"
   170→
   171→    # Build crop expressions for w, h, x, y as ffmpeg expression strings
   172→    w_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "w", total_duration)
   173→    h_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "h", total_duration)
   174→    x_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "x", total_duration)
   175→    y_expr = _build_expr(keyframes, input_w, input_h, "y", total_duration)
   176→
   177→    return f"crop=w='{w_expr}':h='{h_expr}':x='{x_expr}':y='{y_expr}',scale={output_w}:{output_h}"
   178→
   179→
   180→def _build_expr(
   181→    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe],
   182→    input_w: int,
   183→    input_h: int,
   184→    component: str,  # "w", "h", "x", "y"
   185→    total_duration: float,
   186→) -> str:
   187→    """Build an ffmpeg expression string for one crop component.
   188→
   189→    Uses nested if(between(t,start,end), interpolated_val, ...) chains.
   190→    """
   191→    aspect = input_w / input_h
   192→
   193→    def _val(zoom: float, cx: float, cy: float) -> float:
   194→        crop_w = input_w / zoom
   195→        crop_h = input_h / zoom
   196→        crop_x = cx * input_w - crop_w / 2
   197→        crop_y = cy * input_h - crop_h / 2
   198→        # Clamp
   199→        crop_x = max(0, min(crop_x, input_w - crop_w))
   200→        crop_y = max(0, min(crop_y, input_h - crop_h))
   201→        if component == "w":
   202→            return crop_w
   203→        elif component == "h":
   204→            return crop_h
   205→        elif component == "x":
   206→            return crop_x
   207→        elif component == "y":
   208→            return crop_y
   209→        return 0
   210→
   211→    if len(keyframes) == 1:
   212→        kf = keyframes[0]
   213→        return str(int(_val(kf.zoom, kf.x, kf.y)))
   214→
   215→    # Sample the interpolation function at regular intervals and build a
   216→    # piecewise-linear ffmpeg expression. This avoids complex nested
   217→    # if/between logic and correctly handles holds between transitions.
   218→    sample_rate = 10  # samples per second
   219→    n_samples = max(2, int(total_duration * sample_rate) + 1)
   220→    dt = total_duration / (n_samples - 1)
   221→
   222→    samples = []
   223→    for i in range(n_samples):
   224→        t = i * dt
   225→        zoom, cx, cy = _interpolate_keyframes(keyframes, t)
   226→        val = int(round(_val(zoom, cx, cy)))
   227→        samples.append((t, val))
   228→
   229→    # Deduplicate: only keep samples where value changes (run-length encode)
   230→    compressed = [samples[0]]
   231→    for i in range(1, len(samples)):
   232→        if samples[i][1] != samples[i - 1][1]:
   233→            # Ensure we keep the last sample of the previous constant run
   234→            if compressed[-1] != samples[i - 1]:
   235→                compressed.append(samples[i - 1])
   236→            compressed.append(samples[i])
   237→    # Always include the final sample
   238→    if compressed[-1] != samples[-1]:
   239→        compressed.append(samples[-1])
   240→
   241→    # If all samples are the same, return a constant
   242→    if all(s[1] == compressed[0][1] for s in compressed):
   243→        return str(compressed[0][1])
   244→
   245→    # Build ffmpeg expression: chain of if(between(t,start,end), lerp, ...)
   246→    # Work backwards so the else branch is always the next segment
   247→    last_val = compressed[-1][1]
   248→    expr = str(last_val)
   249→
   250→    for i in range(len(compressed) - 2, -1, -1):
   251→        t0, v0 = compressed[i]
   252→        t1, v1 = compressed[i + 1]
   253→        if v0 == v1:
   254→            # Constant segment — hold value
   255→            expr = f"if(between(t,{t0:.3f},{t1:.3f}),{v0},{expr})"
   256→        else:
   257→            # Linear interpolation between samples
   258→            expr = (
   259→                f"if(between(t,{t0:.3f},{t1:.3f}),"
   260→                f"{v0}+({v1}-{v0})*(t-{t0:.3f})/{t1 - t0:.3f}"
   261→                f",{expr})"
   262→            )
   263→
   264→    return expr
   265→
```
