```
1→"""Subprocess wrappers for asciinema recording and agg rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import shutil
     6→import subprocess
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import List, Optional
     9→
    10→
    11→def check_tool(name: str) -> Optional[str]:
    12→    """Return the path to a CLI tool, or None if not found."""
    13→    return shutil.which(name)
    14→
    15→
    16→def require_tool(name: str) -> str:
    17→    """Return the path to a CLI tool, or raise if not found."""
    18→    path = check_tool(name)
    19→    if path is None:
    20→        raise RuntimeError(
    21→            f"'{name}' not found on PATH. Install it first:\n"
    22→            f"  asciinema: pip install asciinema\n"
    23→            f"  agg: cargo install agg (or brew install agg)\n"
    24→            f"  ffmpeg: brew install ffmpeg"
    25→        )
    26→    return path
    27→
    28→
    29→# ── asciinema recording ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    30→
    31→def record_asciinema(
    32→    command: List[str],
    33→    cast_path: Path,
    34→    cols: int = 200,
    35→    rows: int = 50,
    36→    env: Optional[dict] = None,
    37→) -> None:
    38→    """Record a command with asciinema, producing a .cast file.
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        command: The command to record (e.g. ["python", "-m", "demo", "script.yaml", "--play"])
    42→        cast_path: Output path for the .cast file
    43→        cols: Terminal columns
    44→        rows: Terminal rows
    45→        env: Additional environment variables
    46→    """
    47→    asciinema = require_tool("asciinema")
    48→
    49→    cmd = [
    50→        asciinema, "rec",
    51→        "--overwrite",
    52→        f"--cols={cols}",
    53→        f"--rows={rows}",
    54→        "--command", " ".join(command),
    55→        str(cast_path),
    56→    ]
    57→
    58→    import os
    59→    run_env = dict(os.environ)
    60→    run_env["TERM"] = "xterm-256color"
    61→    if env:
    62→        run_env.update(env)
    63→
    64→    result = subprocess.run(
    65→        cmd,
    66→        env=run_env,
    67→        capture_output=True,
    68→        text=True,
    69→    )
    70→    if result.returncode != 0:
    71→        raise RuntimeError(
    72→            f"asciinema recording failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
    73→        )
    74→    if not cast_path.exists():
    75→        raise RuntimeError(f"asciinema did not produce output file: {cast_path}")
    76→
    77→
    78→# ── agg rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    79→
    80→def render_agg(
    81→    cast_path: Path,
    82→    gif_path: Path,
    83→    font_size: int = 22,
    84→    font_family: str = "JetBrains Mono",
    85→    cols: Optional[int] = None,
    86→    rows: Optional[int] = None,
    87→    fps: int = 30,
    88→) -> None:
    89→    """Render a .cast file to a GIF using agg.
    90→
    91→    Note: agg produces GIF. For high-quality video we use agg's raw frame
    92→    output or convert GIF → MP4 with ffmpeg.
    93→    """
    94→    agg = require_tool("agg")
    95→
    96→    cmd = [
    97→        agg,
    98→        f"--font-size={font_size}",
    99→        f"--font-family={font_family}",
   100→        f"--fps-cap={fps}",
   101→    ]
   102→    if cols:
   103→        cmd.append(f"--cols={cols}")
   104→    if rows:
   105→        cmd.append(f"--rows={rows}")
   106→
   107→    cmd.extend([str(cast_path), str(gif_path)])
   108→
   109→    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
   110→    if result.returncode != 0:
   111→        raise RuntimeError(
   112→            f"agg rendering failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
   113→        )
   114→
   115→
   116→# ── ffmpeg encoding ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   117→
   118→def encode_ffmpeg(
   119→    input_path: Path,
   120→    output_path: Path,
   121→    video_filter: Optional[str] = None,
   122→    fps: int = 30,
   123→) -> None:
   124→    """Encode video with ffmpeg, optionally applying a filter chain.
   125→
   126→    Handles both GIF→MP4 and raw frame→MP4 conversions.
   127→    """
   128→    ffmpeg = require_tool("ffmpeg")
   129→
   130→    cmd = [
   131→        ffmpeg, "-y",
   132→        "-i", str(input_path),
   133→    ]
   134→
   135→    if video_filter:
   136→        cmd.extend(["-vf", video_filter])
   137→
   138→    cmd.extend([
   139→        "-c:v", "libx264",
   140→        "-preset", "slow",
   141→        "-crf", "18",
   142→        "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
   143→        "-r", str(fps),
   144→        "-movflags", "+faststart",
   145→        str(output_path),
   146→    ])
   147→
   148→    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
   149→    if result.returncode != 0:
   150→        raise RuntimeError(
   151→            f"ffmpeg encoding failed (exit {result.returncode}):\n{result.stderr}"
   152→        )
   153→
   154→
   155→def get_video_dimensions(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
   156→    """Get width and height of a video/gif file using ffprobe."""
   157→    ffprobe = require_tool("ffprobe")
   158→
   159→    result = subprocess.run(
   160→        [
   161→            ffprobe, "-v", "error",
   162→            "-select_streams", "v:0",
   163→            "-show_entries", "stream=width,height",
   164→            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0",
   165→            str(path),
   166→        ],
   167→        capture_output=True,
   168→        text=True,
   169→    )
   170→    if result.returncode != 0:
   171→        raise RuntimeError(f"ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
   172→
   173→    w, h = result.stdout.strip().split("x")
   174→    return int(w), int(h)
   175→
   176→
   177→def get_video_duration(path: Path) -> float:
   178→    """Get duration of a video/gif file in seconds using ffprobe."""
   179→    ffprobe = require_tool("ffprobe")
   180→
   181→    result = subprocess.run(
   182→        [
   183→            ffprobe, "-v", "error",
   184→            "-show_entries", "format=duration",
   185→            "-of", "csv=s=x:p=0",
   186→            str(path),
   187→        ],
   188→        capture_output=True,
   189→        text=True,
   190→    )
   191→    if result.returncode != 0:
   192→        raise RuntimeError(f"ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
   193→
   194→    return float(result.stdout.strip())
   195→
```
```

1→"""Pydantic models for screenplay YAML and camera directives."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional, Union
     6→
     7→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
     8→
     9→
    10→# ── Camera ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    11→
    12→class CameraDirective(BaseModel):
    13→    """Camera position/zoom keyframe attached to a scene phase or standalone."""
    14→
    15→    zoom: float = 1.0
    16→    x: float = 0.5  # Normalized 0.0–1.0, viewport center
    17→    y: float = 0.5
    18→    at: str = "scene_start"  # Timing marker reference
    19→    duration: float = 0.5  # Transition time in seconds
    20→    ease: Literal["linear", "ease-in", "ease-out", "ease-in-out"] = "ease-in-out"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ── Tool entry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    24→
    25→class ToolEntry(BaseModel):
    26→    """A single tool progress line in a conversation scene."""
    27→
    28→    icon: str = "\u26a1"
    29→    verb: str = "tool"
    30→    detail: str = ""
    31→    duration: str = "0.5s"
    32→    delay: float = 0.15
    33→
    34→
    35→# ── Scene types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    36→
    37→class ConversationScene(BaseModel):
    38→    """A user→thinking→tools→response exchange."""
    39→
    40→    user: str = ""
    41→    tools: List[ToolEntry] = Field(default_factory=list)
    42→    response: str = ""
    43→    response_label: str = " \u2695 Hermes "
    44→    thinking_time: float = 2.0
    45→    typing_speed: Optional[float] = None
    46→    pause_between: Optional[float] = None
    47→    pre_pause: float = 0.5
    48→    post_pause: float = 0.5
    49→
    50→    # Camera directives (Phase 3)
    51→    camera: Optional[CameraDirective] = None
    52→    camera_response: Optional[CameraDirective] = None
    53→
    54→
    55→class ActionScene(BaseModel):
    56→    """A non-conversation action (clear, pause, print, banner, type_command, camera)."""
    57→
    58→    action: str
    59→    # pause
    60→    duration: Optional[float] = None
    61→    # print
    62→    text: Optional[str] = None
    63→    color: Optional[str] = None
    64→    # banner
    65→    model: Optional[str] = None
    66→    context: Optional[str] = None
    67→    session_id: Optional[str] = None
    68→    tools_count: Optional[int] = None
    69→    skills_count: Optional[int] = None
    70→    # type_command
    71→    command: Optional[str] = None
    72→    prefix: Optional[str] = None
    73→    output: Optional[str] = None
    74→    # camera (standalone)
    75→    zoom: Optional[float] = None
    76→    x: Optional[float] = None
    77→    y: Optional[float] = None
    78→    ease: Optional[str] = None
    79→
    80→
    81→Scene = Union[ConversationScene, ActionScene]
    82→
    83→
    84→# ── Output config ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    85→
    86→class OutputConfig(BaseModel):
    87→    """Rendering and encoding settings."""
    88→
    89→    width: int = 2560
    90→    height: int = 1440
    91→    final_width: int = 1280
    92→    final_height: int = 720
    93→    fps: int = 30
    94→    font_size: int = 22
    95→    font_family: str = "Menlo"
    96→
    97→
    98→# ── Top-level screenplay ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    99→
   100→class Screenplay(BaseModel):
   101→    """Complete screenplay document parsed from YAML."""
   102→
   103→    title: str = "Hermes Agent Demo"
   104→    skin: str = "default"
   105→    output: OutputConfig = Field(default_factory=OutputConfig)
   106→    typing_speed: float = 0.04
   107→    pause_between: float = 1.0
   108→    scenes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)
   109→
   110→    # ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   111→
   112→    def parsed_scenes(self) -> List[Scene]:
   113→        """Parse raw scene dicts into typed Scene models."""
   114→        result: List[Scene] = []
   115→        for raw in self.scenes:
   116→            if "action" in raw:
   117→                result.append(ActionScene(**raw))
   118→            else:
   119→                # Convert nested tool dicts to ToolEntry models
   120→                tools_raw = raw.get("tools", [])
   121→                tools = [ToolEntry(**t) if isinstance(t, dict) else t for t in tools_raw]
   122→                data = {**raw, "tools": tools}
   123→                # Parse camera sub-objects
   124→                if "camera" in data and isinstance(data["camera"], dict):
   125→                    data["camera"] = CameraDirective(**data["camera"])
   126→                if "camera_response" in data and isinstance(data["camera_response"], dict):
   127→                    data["camera_response"] = CameraDirective(**data["camera_response"])
   128→                result.append(ConversationScene(**data))
   129→        return result
   130→
```

---

## Implementation Plan: Python Frame-Based Camera Processing

### Problem Summary

The current pipeline generates ffmpeg filter expressions that use the `t` (time) variable for dynamic crop/pan/zoom. This fails because ffmpeg's `t` variable does not work correctly with GIF input -- GIF frames lack proper PTS timestamps, so `t` is always 0 or undefined. The interpolation math in `_interpolate_keyframes` and `_ease` is correct and working; only the delivery mechanism (ffmpeg expressions) is broken.

### Architecture Decision

Create a single new module `demo/compositor.py` that:
1. Reads the GIF frame-by-frame using PIL (Pillow)
2. Crops each frame according to the interpolated camera state at that frame's timestamp
3. Resizes the crop to the output resolution
4. Pipes raw RGB frames to ffmpeg via stdin for encoding

PIL/Pillow is the right choice over imageio because:
- Pillow is a near-universal Python dependency (already likely installed transitively)
- `PIL.Image.open()` with `.seek()` and `.tell()` handles animated GIF frame iteration natively
- GIF frame durations are accessible via `frame.info["duration"]` (in milliseconds)
- Pillow's `.crop()` and `.resize()` with `LANCZOS` resampling are exactly what we need
- No additional dependency beyond Pillow

### Step-by-Step Plan

#### 1. Create `demo/compositor.py` (new file, ~80 lines)

This module contains one public function:

```python
def composite_frames(
    gif_path: Path,
    output_path: Path,
    keyframes: List[CameraKeyframe],
    output_w: int,
    output_h: int,
    fps: int = 30,
) -> None:
```

Implementation details:

**a) Read GIF frames and compute timestamps.**
Use PIL to iterate all frames. GIF stores per-frame `duration` in milliseconds in `frame.info["duration"]`. Accumulate to get each frame's absolute timestamp.

```python
from PIL import Image

img = Image.open(gif_path)
frames = []
t = 0.0
while True:
    frame = img.copy().convert("RGB")
    duration_ms = img.info.get("duration", 33)  # fallback ~30fps
    frames.append((t, frame))
    t += duration_ms / 1000.0
    try:
        img.seek(img.tell() + 1)
    except EOFError:
        break
total_duration = t
```

**b) For each frame, compute crop region.**
Reuse `_interpolate_keyframes` from `camera.py` to get `(zoom, x, y)` at each frame's timestamp. Convert normalized coordinates to pixel crop box:

```python
from demo.camera import _interpolate_keyframes

zoom, cx, cy = _interpolate_keyframes(keyframes, frame_t)
input_w, input_h = frame.size
crop_w = input_w / zoom
crop_h = input_h / zoom
crop_x = cx * input_w - crop_w / 2
crop_y = cy * input_h - crop_h / 2
# Clamp to bounds
crop_x = max(0, min(crop_x, input_w - crop_w))
crop_y = max(0, min(crop_y, input_h - crop_h))
cropped = frame.crop((int(crop_x), int(crop_y), int(crop_x + crop_w), int(crop_y + crop_h)))
result = cropped.resize((output_w, output_h), Image.LANCZOS)
```

**c) Pipe to ffmpeg.**
Open an ffmpeg subprocess reading rawvideo from stdin:

```python
ffmpeg_cmd = [
    "ffmpeg", "-y",
    "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24",
    "-s", f"{output_w}x{output_h}",
    "-r", str(fps),
    "-i", "pipe:0",
    "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "slow", "-crf", "18",
    "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
    "-movflags", "+faststart",
    str(output_path),
]
proc = subprocess.Popen(ffmpeg_cmd, stdin=subprocess.PIPE)
```

Then for each processed frame, write `result.tobytes()` to `proc.stdin`. Close stdin when done and wait for the process.

**d) Handle frame rate conversion.**
The GIF has variable frame durations, but we want constant-fps output. Two options:
- **Simple (recommended):** Just feed GIF frames as-is at their native rate. Set ffmpeg input `-r` to match the average GIF fps. Since agg already caps at the configured fps, this is good enough.
- **Precise:** Resample to exact constant fps by duplicating/dropping frames. For a first pass, the simple approach is fine since agg is already fps-capped.

The simple approach: compute `total_duration` from accumulated frame durations, tell ffmpeg `-r {fps}` for output, and `-r {len(frames)/total_duration}` for input. ffmpeg handles the resampling.

Actually, even simpler: write each frame for a number of times proportional to its display duration at the target fps. For frame with duration_ms, write it `round(duration_ms / 1000 * fps)` times (minimum 1). This gives exact constant-fps output with no ffmpeg framerate conversion needed.

#### 2. Modify `demo/camera.py`

**Remove** (lines 151-264):
- `generate_ffmpeg_filter()` function
- `_build_expr()` function

**Keep** (lines 1-148):
- `CameraKeyframe` dataclass
- `resolve_keyframes()` function
- `_resolve_marker_time()` helper
- `_ease()` function
- `_interpolate_keyframes()` function

Make `_interpolate_keyframes` public by renaming to `interpolate_keyframes` (drop the underscore), since `compositor.py` will import it. Also make `_ease` available but it does not need to be public since `interpolate_keyframes` calls it internally.

#### 3. Modify `demo/pipeline.py`

**Step 3 changes:** After resolving keyframes and scaling timestamps (keep that logic, lines 91-131), instead of calling `generate_ffmpeg_filter`, call the new compositor:

```python
from demo.compositor import composite_frames
composite_frames(
    gif_path=gif_path,
    output_path=output_path,
    keyframes=keyframes,
    output_w=out.final_width,
    output_h=out.final_height,
    fps=out.fps,
)
```

**Remove Step 4** as a separate step. The compositor handles both the camera and the encoding in one pass. Steps become:
1. Record with asciinema (unchanged)
2. Render with agg (unchanged)
3. Apply camera and encode (compositor does both)

**No-camera fallback:** When there are no keyframes (no timing manifest), the compositor still works -- `_interpolate_keyframes` with the default t=0 keyframe (zoom=1.0, center) just passes through every frame at full size, scaled to output dimensions. So the same codepath handles both cases.

**Remove imports** of `generate_ffmpeg_filter` and `encode_ffmpeg`. Keep imports of `resolve_keyframes`, `render_agg`, `record_asciinema`, `get_video_dimensions`, `get_video_duration`.

#### 4. Modify `demo/recorder.py`

**Remove** `encode_ffmpeg()` function (lines 118-152). It is no longer needed since the compositor handles encoding.

**Keep** everything else: `check_tool`, `require_tool`, `record_asciinema`, `render_agg`, `get_video_dimensions`, `get_video_duration`.

#### 5. Dependencies

Add `Pillow` to an optional dependency group in `pyproject.toml`:

```toml
demo = ["Pillow>=9.0"]
```

Or just add it to the import with a helpful error message in `compositor.py`:

```python
try:
    from PIL import Image
except ImportError:
    raise ImportError("Pillow is required for demo video compositing: pip install Pillow")
```

The lazy import with a clear error message is preferable since the demo pipeline is a development tool, not a production dependency.

#### 6. Update `_print_dry_run` in `pipeline.py`

Update the dry run output to reflect the new pipeline (steps 3+4 merged, no ffmpeg filter expression shown, describe the frame-by-frame compositor approach instead).

### Potential Gotchas

1. **GIF palette/transparency:** `img.copy().convert("RGB")` handles palette-mode GIFs correctly. The `.copy()` is essential because PIL reuses the image buffer when seeking.

2. **Memory:** For a 30-second video at 30fps with 2560x1440 frames, that is ~900 frames. Each RGB frame is ~11MB, so holding all frames in memory costs ~10GB. Instead of loading all frames first, process them in a streaming fashion: iterate the GIF, crop, write to ffmpeg pipe, discard the frame. This keeps memory at O(1 frame).

3. **GIF frame disposal:** Some GIFs use incremental rendering where each frame is a diff. PIL handles this correctly when you call `.seek()` sequentially, but you must call `.copy()` before seeking to the next frame, which the plan already does.

4. **Even dimensions for H.264:** `output_w` and `output_h` must be even for yuv420p. The existing `OutputConfig` defaults (1280x720) are fine, but add a guard: `output_w &= ~1; output_h &= ~1`.

### Summary of Changes

| File | Action |
|------|--------|
| `demo/compositor.py` | **CREATE** -- ~80 lines, one function |
| `demo/camera.py` | **EDIT** -- Remove `generate_ffmpeg_filter` and `_build_expr` (~115 lines). Rename `_interpolate_keyframes` to `interpolate_keyframes`. |
| `demo/pipeline.py` | **EDIT** -- Replace step 3 filter generation + step 4 encode with single `composite_frames` call. Update imports, step numbering, dry run. |
| `demo/recorder.py` | **EDIT** -- Remove `encode_ffmpeg` function (~35 lines). |
| `pyproject.toml` | **EDIT** -- Optionally add `demo = ["Pillow>=9.0"]` to `[project.optional-dependencies]`. |

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/demo/compositor.py` - New module to create: GIF frame reader, camera crop, ffmpeg pipe
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/demo/pipeline.py` - Orchestrator to rewire: replace filter+encode steps with compositor call
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/demo/camera.py` - Remove ffmpeg expression code (lines 151-264), make `_interpolate_keyframes` public
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/demo/recorder.py` - Remove `encode_ffmpeg` (lines 118-152)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/demo/schema.py` - Reference only, no changes needed, but contains OutputConfig dimensions used by compositor

```
1→"""Hermes CLI skin/theme engine.
     2→
     3→A data-driven skin system that lets users customize the CLI's visual appearance.
     4→Skins are defined as YAML files in ~/.hermes/skins/ or as built-in presets.
     5→No code changes are needed to add a new skin.
     6→
     7→SKIN YAML SCHEMA
     8→================
     9→
    10→All fields are optional. Missing values inherit from the ``default`` skin.
    11→
    12→.. code-block:: yaml
    13→
    14→    # Required: skin identity
    15→    name: mytheme                         # Unique skin name (lowercase, hyphens ok)
    16→    description: Short description        # Shown in /skin listing
    17→
    18→    # Colors: hex values for Rich markup (banner, UI, response box)
    19→    # These are the default (dark terminal) colors.
    20→    colors:
    21→      banner_border: "#CD7F32"            # Panel border color
    22→      banner_title: "#FFD700"             # Panel title text color
    23→      banner_accent: "#FFBF00"            # Section headers (Available Tools, etc.)
    24→      banner_dim: "#B8860B"               # Dim/muted text (separators, labels)
    25→      banner_text: "#FFF8DC"              # Body text (tool names, skill names)
    26→      ui_accent: "#FFBF00"               # General UI accent
    27→      ui_label: "#4dd0e1"                # UI labels
    28→      ui_ok: "#4caf50"                   # Success indicators
    29→      ui_error: "#ef5350"                # Error indicators
    30→      ui_warn: "#ffa726"                 # Warning indicators
    31→      prompt: "#FFF8DC"                  # Prompt text color
    32→      input_rule: "#CD7F32"              # Input area horizontal rule
    33→      response_border: "#FFD700"         # Response box border (ANSI)
    34→      session_label: "#DAA520"           # Session label color
    35→      session_border: "#8B8682"          # Session ID dim color
    36→
    37→    # Light-mode color overrides (optional). Only keys that need to change.
    38→    # When theme_mode is "light", these replace the corresponding colors above.
    39→    colors_light:
    40→      banner_title: "#B8860B"
    41→      prompt: "#5C4A00"
    42→
    43→    # Spinner: customize the animated spinner during API calls
    44→    spinner:
    45→      waiting_faces:                      # Faces shown while waiting for API
    46→        - "(⚔)"
    47→        - "(⛨)"
    48→      thinking_faces:                     # Faces shown during reasoning
    49→        - "(⌁)"
    50→        - "(<>)"
    51→      thinking_verbs:                     # Verbs for spinner messages
    52→        - "forging"
    53→        - "plotting"
    54→      wings:                              # Optional left/right spinner decorations
    55→        - ["⟪⚔", "⚔⟫"]                  # Each entry is [left, right] pair
    56→        - ["⟪▲", "▲⟫"]
    57→
    58→    # Branding: text strings used throughout the CLI
    59→    branding:
    60→      agent_name: "Hermes Agent"          # Banner title, status display
    61→      welcome: "Welcome message"          # Shown at CLI startup
    62→      goodbye: "Goodbye! ⚕"              # Shown on exit
    63→      response_label: " ⚕ Hermes "       # Response box header label
    64→      prompt_symbol: "❯ "                # Input prompt symbol
    65→      help_header: "(^_^)? Commands"      # /help header text
    66→
    67→    # Tool prefix: character for tool output lines (default: ┊)
    68→    tool_prefix: "┊"
    69→
    70→USAGE
    71→=====
    72→
    73→.. code-block:: python
    74→
    75→    from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin, list_skins, set_active_skin
    76→
    77→    skin = get_active_skin()
    78→    print(skin.colors["banner_title"])    # "#FFD700"
    79→    print(skin.get_branding("agent_name"))  # "Hermes Agent"
    80→
    81→    set_active_skin("ares")               # Switch to built-in ares skin
    82→    set_active_skin("mytheme")            # Switch to user skin from ~/.hermes/skins/
    83→
    84→BUILT-IN SKINS
    85→==============
    86→
    87→- ``default`` — Classic Hermes gold/kawaii (the current look)
    88→- ``ares``    — Crimson/bronze war-god theme with custom spinner wings
    89→- ``mono``    — Clean grayscale monochrome
    90→- ``slate``   — Cool blue developer-focused theme
    91→
    92→USER SKINS
    93→==========
    94→
    95→Drop a YAML file in ``~/.hermes/skins/<name>.yaml`` following the schema above.
    96→Activate with ``/skin <name>`` in the CLI or ``display.skin: <name>`` in config.yaml.
    97→"""
    98→
    99→import logging
   100→import os
   101→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   102→from pathlib import Path
   103→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
   104→
   105→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   106→
   107→
   108→# =============================================================================
   109→# Skin data structure
   110→# =============================================================================
   111→
   112→@dataclass
   113→class SkinConfig:
   114→    """Complete skin configuration."""
   115→    name: str
   116→    description: str = ""
   117→    colors: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   118→    colors_light: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   119→    spinner: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   120→    branding: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   121→    tool_prefix: str = "┊"
   122→    banner_logo: str = ""    # Rich-markup ASCII art logo (replaces HERMES_AGENT_LOGO)
   123→    banner_hero: str = ""    # Rich-markup hero art (replaces HERMES_CADUCEUS)
   124→
   125→    def get_color(self, key: str, fallback: str = "") -> str:
   126→        """Get a color value with fallback, respecting the active theme mode.
   127→
   128→        If the active theme mode is "light" and a light-mode override exists
   129→        for this key, return it. Otherwise return the default (dark) color.
   130→        """
   131→        mode = get_theme_mode()
   132→        if mode == "light" and key in self.colors_light:
   133→            return self.colors_light[key]
   134→        return self.colors.get(key, fallback)
   135→
   136→    def get_spinner_list(self, key: str) -> List[str]:
   137→        """Get a spinner list (faces, verbs, etc.)."""
   138→        return self.spinner.get(key, [])
   139→
   140→    def get_spinner_wings(self) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
   141→        """Get spinner wing pairs, or empty list if none."""
   142→        raw = self.spinner.get("wings", [])
   143→        result = []
   144→        for pair in raw:
   145→            if isinstance(pair, (list, tuple)) and len(pair) == 2:
   146→                result.append((str(pair[0]), str(pair[1])))
   147→        return result
   148→
   149→    def get_branding(self, key: str, fallback: str = "") -> str:
   150→        """Get a branding value with fallback."""
   151→        return self.branding.get(key, fallback)
   152→
   153→
   154→# =============================================================================
   155→# Built-in skin definitions
   156→# =============================================================================
   157→
   158→_BUILTIN_SKINS: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {
   159→    "default": {
   160→        "name": "default",
   161→        "description": "Classic Hermes — gold and kawaii",
   162→        "colors": {
   163→            "banner_border": "#CD7F32",
   164→            "banner_title": "#FFD700",
   165→            "banner_accent": "#FFBF00",
   166→            "banner_dim": "#B8860B",
   167→            "banner_text": "#FFF8DC",
   168→            "ui_accent": "#FFBF00",
   169→            "ui_label": "#4dd0e1",
   170→            "ui_ok": "#4caf50",
   171→            "ui_error": "#ef5350",
   172→            "ui_warn": "#ffa726",
   173→            "prompt": "#FFF8DC",
   174→            "input_rule": "#CD7F32",
   175→            "response_border": "#FFD700",
   176→            "session_label": "#DAA520",
   177→            "session_border": "#8B8682",
   178→        },
   179→        "colors_light": {
   180→            "banner_border": "#7A5A00",
   181→            "banner_title": "#6B4C00",
   182→            "banner_accent": "#7A5500",
   183→            "banner_dim": "#8B7355",
   184→            "banner_text": "#3D2B00",
   185→            "prompt": "#3D2B00",
   186→            "ui_accent": "#7A5500",

   611→[#C75B1D]⢸⡟⠀⠀⣿⡟⠀⠀⠀🔥⠀⠀⠀⠀⢻⣿⠀⠀⢻⡇[/]

   613→[#7A3511]⠀⠀⠈⠻⣦⣀⠙⢿⣷⣤⣤⣤⣾⡿⠋⣀⣴⠟⠁⠀⠀[/]

   616→[#F29C38]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣼⡟⠀⠀⢻⣧⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]

   621→
   622→# =============================================================================
   623→# Skin loading and management
   624→# =============================================================================
   625→
   626→_active_skin: Optional[SkinConfig] = None
   627→_active_skin_name: str = "default"
   628→_theme_mode: str = "auto"  # "auto", "light", or "dark"
   629→_resolved_theme_mode: Optional[str] = None  # Cached result of auto-detection
   630→
   631→
   632→def _skins_dir() -> Path:
   633→    """User skins directory."""
   634→    home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   635→    return home / "skins"
   636→
   637→
   638→def _load_skin_from_yaml(path: Path) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   639→    """Load a skin definition from a YAML file."""
   640→    try:
   641→        import yaml
   642→        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   643→            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
   644→        if isinstance(data, dict) and "name" in data:
   645→            return data
   646→    except Exception as e:
   647→        logger.debug("Failed to load skin from %s: %s", path, e)
   648→    return None
   649→
   650→
   651→def _build_skin_config(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> SkinConfig:
   652→    """Build a SkinConfig from a raw dict (built-in or loaded from YAML)."""
   653→    # Start with default values as base for missing keys
   654→    default = _BUILTIN_SKINS["default"]
   655→    colors = dict(default.get("colors", {}))
   656→    colors.update(data.get("colors", {}))
   657→    colors_light = dict(default.get("colors_light", {}))
   658→    colors_light.update(data.get("colors_light", {}))
   659→    spinner = dict(default.get("spinner", {}))
   660→    spinner.update(data.get("spinner", {}))
   661→    branding = dict(default.get("branding", {}))
   662→    branding.update(data.get("branding", {}))
   663→
   664→    return SkinConfig(
   665→        name=data.get("name", "unknown"),
   666→        description=data.get("description", ""),
   667→        colors=colors,
   668→        colors_light=colors_light,
   669→        spinner=spinner,
   670→        branding=branding,
   671→        tool_prefix=data.get("tool_prefix", default.get("tool_prefix", "┊")),
   672→        banner_logo=data.get("banner_logo", ""),
   673→        banner_hero=data.get("banner_hero", ""),
   674→    )
   675→
   676→
   677→def list_skins() -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
   678→    """List all available skins (built-in + user-installed).
   679→
   680→    Returns list of {"name": ..., "description": ..., "source": "builtin"|"user"}.
   681→    """
   682→    result = []
   683→    for name, data in _BUILTIN_SKINS.items():
   684→        result.append({
   685→            "name": name,
   686→            "description": data.get("description", ""),
   687→            "source": "builtin",
   688→        })
   689→
   690→    skins_path = _skins_dir()
   691→    if skins_path.is_dir():
   692→        for f in sorted(skins_path.glob("*.yaml")):
   693→            data = _load_skin_from_yaml(f)
   694→            if data:
   695→                skin_name = data.get("name", f.stem)
   696→                # Skip if it shadows a built-in
   697→                if any(s["name"] == skin_name for s in result):
   698→                    continue
   699→                result.append({
   700→                    "name": skin_name,
   701→                    "description": data.get("description", ""),
   702→                    "source": "user",
   703→                })
   704→
   705→    return result
   706→
   707→
   708→def load_skin(name: str) -> SkinConfig:
   709→    """Load a skin by name. Checks user skins first, then built-in."""
   710→    # Check user skins directory
   711→    skins_path = _skins_dir()
   712→    user_file = skins_path / f"{name}.yaml"
   713→    if user_file.is_file():
   714→        data = _load_skin_from_yaml(user_file)
   715→        if data:
   716→            return _build_skin_config(data)
   717→
   718→    # Check built-in skins
   719→    if name in _BUILTIN_SKINS:
   720→        return _build_skin_config(_BUILTIN_SKINS[name])
   721→
   722→    # Fallback to default
   723→    logger.warning("Skin '%s' not found, using default", name)
   724→    return _build_skin_config(_BUILTIN_SKINS["default"])
   725→
   726→
   727→def get_theme_mode() -> str:
   728→    """Get the resolved theme mode ("light" or "dark").
   729→
   730→    If set to "auto", detects the terminal background on first call and
   731→    caches the result in memory for the session. Falls back to "dark" if
   732→    detection fails.
   733→    """
   734→    global _resolved_theme_mode
   735→    if _theme_mode in ("light", "dark"):
   736→        return _theme_mode
   737→    # Auto-detect
   738→    if _resolved_theme_mode is None:
   739→        try:
   740→            from hermes_cli.colors import detect_terminal_background
   741→            result = detect_terminal_background()
   742→            _resolved_theme_mode = result if result != "unknown" else "dark"
   743→        except Exception:
   744→            _resolved_theme_mode = "dark"
   745→    return _resolved_theme_mode
   746→
   747→
   748→def get_theme_mode_setting() -> str:
   749→    """Get the raw theme_mode setting ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   750→    return _theme_mode
   751→
   752→
   753→def set_theme_mode(mode: str) -> None:
   754→    """Set the theme mode ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   755→    global _theme_mode, _resolved_theme_mode
   756→    if mode not in ("auto", "light", "dark"):
   757→        mode = "auto"
   758→    _theme_mode = mode
   759→    _resolved_theme_mode = None  # Reset cache so auto re-detects
   760→
   761→
   762→def get_active_skin() -> SkinConfig:
   763→    """Get the currently active skin config (cached)."""
   764→    global _active_skin
   765→    if _active_skin is None:
   766→        _active_skin = load_skin(_active_skin_name)
   767→    return _active_skin
   768→
   769→
   770→def set_active_skin(name: str) -> SkinConfig:
   771→    """Switch the active skin. Returns the new SkinConfig."""
   772→    global _active_skin, _active_skin_name
   773→    _active_skin_name = name
   774→    _active_skin = load_skin(name)
   775→    return _active_skin
   776→
   777→
   778→def get_active_skin_name() -> str:
   779→    """Get the name of the currently active skin."""
   780→    return _active_skin_name
   781→
   782→
   783→def init_skin_from_config(config: dict) -> None:
   784→    """Initialize the active skin and theme mode from CLI config at startup.
   785→
   786→    Call this once during CLI init with the loaded config dict.
   787→    When ``theme_mode`` is ``auto``, detection happens lazily on the first
   788→    ``get_theme_mode()`` call so we always reflect the current terminal.
   789→    """
   790→    display = config.get("display", {})
   791→    skin_name = display.get("skin", "default")
   792→    if isinstance(skin_name, str) and skin_name.strip():
   793→        set_active_skin(skin_name.strip())
   794→    else:
   795→        set_active_skin("default")
   796→
   797→    theme_mode = display.get("theme_mode", "auto")
   798→    if isinstance(theme_mode, str) and theme_mode.strip():
   799→        set_theme_mode(theme_mode.strip())
   800→    else:
   801→        set_theme_mode("auto")
   802→
```

```
1→"""Scene registry, PlaybackContext, and base protocol."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import sys
     7→import time
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Protocol
    11→
    12→from rich.console import Console
    13→
    14→from hermes_cli.skin_engine import SkinConfig, load_skin
    15→
    16→
    17→# ── Timing manifest ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class TimingMarker:
    21→    """A single named timestamp in the recording."""
    22→    name: str
    23→    t: float
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass
    27→class SceneTiming:
    28→    """Timing data for one scene."""
    29→    index: int
    30→    scene_type: str  # "conversation" | "action"
    31→    action: Optional[str] = None
    32→    start_t: float = 0.0
    33→    end_t: float = 0.0
    34→    markers: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass
    38→class TimingManifest:
    39→    """Full timing data for a recording session."""
    40→    scenes: List[SceneTiming] = field(default_factory=list)
    41→    total_duration: float = 0.0
    42→
    43→    def add_scene(self, scene: SceneTiming):
    44→        self.scenes.append(scene)
    45→
    46→    def finalize(self):
    47→        if self.scenes:
    48→            self.total_duration = max(s.end_t for s in self.scenes)
    49→
    50→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    51→        result = {"scenes": [], "total_duration": self.total_duration}
    52→        for s in self.scenes:
    53→            entry: Dict[str, Any] = {
    54→                "index": s.index,
    55→                "type": s.scene_type,
    56→                "start_t": round(s.start_t, 3),
    57→                "end_t": round(s.end_t, 3),
    58→            }
    59→            if s.action:
    60→                entry["action"] = s.action
    61→            if s.markers:
    62→                entry["markers"] = {k: round(v, 3) for k, v in s.markers.items()}
    63→            result["scenes"].append(entry)
    64→        return result
    65→
    66→    def save(self, path: Path):
    67→        path.write_text(json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=2))
    68→
    69→
    70→# ── ANSI helpers (skin-aware) ───────────────────────────────────────────
    71→
    72→def hex_to_ansi(hex_color: str) -> str:
    73→    """Convert #RRGGBB to ANSI 24-bit foreground escape."""
    74→    h = hex_color.lstrip("#")
    75→    if len(h) != 6:
    76→        return ""
    77→    r, g, b = int(h[0:2], 16), int(h[2:4], 16), int(h[4:6], 16)
    78→    return f"\033[38;2;{r};{g};{b}m"
    79→
    80→
    81→_BOLD = "\033[1m"
    82→_DIM = "\033[2m"
    83→_RST = "\033[0m"
    84→
    85→
    86→# ── Playback context ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    87→
    88→@dataclass
    89→class PlaybackContext:
    90→    """Shared state passed to every scene handler during playback."""
    91→
    92→    skin: SkinConfig
    93→    console: Console
    94→    typing_speed: float = 0.04
    95→    pause_between: float = 1.0
    96→    scene_index: int = 0
    97→    recording_start: float = 0.0  # monotonic time of recording start
    98→    timing: Optional[TimingManifest] = None
    99→
   100→    # ── skin ANSI shortcuts ──────────────────────────────────────────
   101→
   102→    @property
   103→    def ansi_prompt(self) -> str:
   104→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("prompt", "#FFF8DC"))
   105→
   106→    @property
   107→    def ansi_banner_title(self) -> str:
   108→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_title", "#FFD700"))
   109→
   110→    @property
   111→    def ansi_banner_border(self) -> str:
   112→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32"))
   113→
   114→    @property
   115→    def ansi_banner_accent(self) -> str:
   116→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00"))
   117→
   118→    @property
   119→    def ansi_banner_dim(self) -> str:
   120→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B"))
   121→
   122→    @property
   123→    def ansi_banner_text(self) -> str:
   124→        return hex_to_ansi(self.skin.get_color("banner_text", "#FFF8DC"))
   125→
   126→    # ── timing helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   127→
   128→    def now(self) -> float:
   129→        """Seconds since recording started."""
   130→        return time.monotonic() - self.recording_start
   131→
   132→    def mark(self, scene_timing: SceneTiming, name: str):
   133→        """Record a named timing marker on the current scene."""
   134→        scene_timing.markers[name] = self.now()
   135→
   136→
   137→# ── Scene handler protocol ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   138→
   139→class SceneHandler(Protocol):
   140→    """Protocol for scene handler functions."""
   141→
   142→    def __call__(self, scene: Any, ctx: PlaybackContext) -> None: ...
   143→
   144→
   145→# ── Scene registry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   146→
   147→_registry: Dict[str, SceneHandler] = {}
   148→
   149→
   150→def register(scene_type: str) -> Callable:
   151→    """Decorator to register a scene handler."""
   152→    def decorator(fn: SceneHandler) -> SceneHandler:
   153→        _registry[scene_type] = fn
   154→        return fn
   155→    return decorator
   156→
   157→
   158→def get_handler(scene_type: str) -> Optional[SceneHandler]:
   159→    """Look up a registered scene handler."""
   160→    return _registry.get(scene_type)
   161→
   162→
   163→def dispatch(scene: Any, ctx: PlaybackContext) -> None:
   164→    """Dispatch a parsed scene to its handler."""
   165→    from demo.schema import ActionScene, ConversationScene
   166→
   167→    if isinstance(scene, ActionScene):
   168→        handler = get_handler(f"action:{scene.action}")
   169→        if handler is None:
   170→            handler = get_handler("action")
   171→        if handler:
   172→            handler(scene, ctx)
   173→    elif isinstance(scene, ConversationScene):
   174→        handler = get_handler("conversation")
   175→        if handler:
   176→            handler(scene, ctx)
   177→
```

```
1→"""Conversation scene: user prompt -> spinner -> tools -> response."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import random
     6→import sys
     7→import threading
     8→import time
     9→
    10→from rich.markdown import Markdown
    11→from rich.panel import Panel
    12→from rich import box as rich_box
    13→
    14→from demo.schema import ConversationScene
    15→from demo.scenes import (
    16→    PlaybackContext, SceneTiming, _BOLD, _DIM, _RST,
    17→    hex_to_ansi, register,
    18→)
    19→
    20→# ── Spinner frames ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    21→
    22→SPINNER_FRAMES = ["\u280b", "\u2819", "\u2839", "\u2838", "\u283c", "\u2834", "\u2826", "\u2827", "\u2807", "\u280f"]
    23→
    24→# Defaults (used when skin has none)
    25→_DEFAULT_THINKING_FACES = [
    26→    "(｡•́︿•̀｡)", "(◔_◔)", "(¬‿¬)", "( •_•)>⌐■-■", "(⌐■_■)",
    27→    "(´･_･`)", "◉_◉", "(°ロ°)", "( ˘⌣˘)♡", "ヽ(>∀<☆)☆",
    28→    "٩(๑❛ᴗ❛๑)۶", "(⊙_⊙)", "(¬_¬)", "( ͡° ͜ʖ ͡°)", "ಠ_ಠ",
    29→]
    30→
    31→_DEFAULT_THINKING_VERBS = [
    32→    "pondering", "contemplating", "musing", "cogitating", "ruminating",
    33→    "deliberating", "mulling", "reflecting", "processing", "reasoning",
    34→    "analyzing", "computing", "synthesizing", "formulating", "brainstorming",
    35→]
    36→
    37→
    38→def _get_faces(ctx: PlaybackContext) -> list:
    39→    faces = ctx.skin.get_spinner_list("thinking_faces")
    40→    return faces if faces else _DEFAULT_THINKING_FACES
    41→
    42→
    43→def _get_verbs(ctx: PlaybackContext) -> list:
    44→    verbs = ctx.skin.get_spinner_list("thinking_verbs")
    45→    return verbs if verbs else _DEFAULT_THINKING_VERBS
    46→
    47→
    48→# ── Typing simulation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    49→
    50→def type_text(text: str, speed: float, color: str):
    51→    """Simulate typing character by character."""
    52→    for ch in text:
    53→        sys.stdout.write(f"{color}{ch}{_RST}")
    54→        sys.stdout.flush()
    55→        if ch in " \t":
    56→            time.sleep(speed * 0.5)
    57→        elif ch in ".,!?;:":
    58→            time.sleep(speed * 2.5)
    59→        else:
    60→            time.sleep(speed * (0.7 + random.random() * 0.6))
    61→
    62→
    63→# ── Demo spinner ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    64→
    65→class DemoSpinner:
    66→    """Animated braille spinner with kawaii faces."""
    67→
    68→    def __init__(self, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    69→        self.ctx = ctx
    70→        self.running = False
    71→        self.thread = None
    72→        self.start_time = 0.0
    73→        self.frame_idx = 0
    74→        self.message = ""
    75→        self.last_len = 0
    76→        self._wings = ctx.skin.get_spinner_wings()
    77→
    78→    def start(self, message: str = ""):
    79→        self.message = message
    80→        self.running = True
    81→        self.start_time = time.time()
    82→        self.frame_idx = 0
    83→        self.last_len = 0
    84→        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._animate, daemon=True)
    85→        self.thread.start()
    86→
    87→    def _animate(self):
    88→        while self.running:
    89→            frame = SPINNER_FRAMES[self.frame_idx % len(SPINNER_FRAMES)]
    90→            elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time
    91→            if self._wings:
    92→                left, right = self._wings[self.frame_idx % len(self._wings)]
    93→                line = f"  {left} {frame} {self.message} {right} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
    94→            else:
    95→                line = f"  {frame} {self.message} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
    96→            pad = max(self.last_len - len(line), 0)
    97→            sys.stdout.write(f"\r{line}{' ' * pad}")
    98→            sys.stdout.flush()
    99→            self.last_len = len(line)
   100→            self.frame_idx += 1
   101→            time.sleep(0.12)
   102→
   103→    def update(self, message: str):
   104→        self.message = message
   105→
   106→    def stop(self):
   107→        self.running = False
   108→        if self.thread:
   109→            self.thread.join(timeout=0.5)
   110→        blanks = " " * max(self.last_len + 5, 40)
   111→        sys.stdout.write(f"\r{blanks}\r")
   112→        sys.stdout.flush()
   113→
   114→
   115→# ── Tool progress lines ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   116→
   117→def show_tool_line(tool: dict, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   118→    """Print a tool progress line."""
   119→    icon = tool.get("icon", "\u26a1")
   120→    verb = tool.get("verb", "tool")
   121→    detail = tool.get("detail", "")
   122→    duration = tool.get("duration", "0.5s")
   123→    prefix = ctx.skin.tool_prefix
   124→
   125→    line = f"  {prefix} {icon} {verb:9} {detail}  {duration}"
   126→    sys.stdout.write(f"{_DIM}{line}{_RST}\n")
   127→    sys.stdout.flush()
   128→
   129→
   130→# ── Response rendering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   131→
   132→def show_response(text: str, label: str, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   133→    """Show agent response in a Rich panel using skin colors."""
   134→    border_color = ctx.skin.get_color("response_border", "#FFD700")
   135→    ctx.console.print(Panel(
   136→        Markdown(text),
   137→        title=f"[bold]{label}[/bold]",
   138→        title_align="left",
   139→        border_style=border_color,
   140→        box=rich_box.HORIZONTALS,
   141→        padding=(1, 2),
   142→    ))
   143→
   144→
   145→# ── Scene handler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   146→
   147→@register("conversation")
   148→def play_conversation(scene: ConversationScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   149→    """Play a conversation scene: user → spinner → tools → response."""
   150→    typing_speed = scene.typing_speed or ctx.typing_speed
   151→    prompt_color = ctx.ansi_prompt
   152→    prompt_symbol = ctx.skin.get_branding("prompt_symbol", "\u276f ")
   153→    response_label = scene.response_label or ctx.skin.get_branding("response_label", " \u2695 Hermes ")
   154→
   155→    # Timing
   156→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
   157→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="conversation"
   158→    )
   159→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
   160→
   161→    # --- User message ---
   162→    if scene.user:
   163→        time.sleep(scene.pre_pause)
   164→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "user_start")
   165→
   166→        # Show prompt
   167→        sys.stdout.write(f"\n{prompt_color}{prompt_symbol}{_RST}")
   168→        sys.stdout.flush()
   169→        time.sleep(0.3)
   170→
   171→        # Type the message
   172→        type_text(scene.user, speed=typing_speed, color=ctx.ansi_banner_text)
   173→        time.sleep(0.6)
   174→
   175→        # "Submit" — show as gold bullet
   176→        gold = hex_to_ansi(ctx.skin.get_color("banner_title", "#FFD700"))
   177→        sys.stdout.write(f"\r\033[K")
   178→        sys.stdout.write(f"{gold}\u25cf {_BOLD}{scene.user}{_RST}\n")
   179→        sys.stdout.flush()
   180→        time.sleep(0.3)
   181→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "user_end")
   182→
   183→    # --- Spinner (thinking) ---
   184→    ctx.mark(scene_timing, "thinking_start")
   185→    spinner = DemoSpinner(ctx)
   186→    face = random.choice(_get_faces(ctx))
   187→    verb = random.choice(_get_verbs(ctx))
   188→    spinner.start(f"{face} {verb}...")
   189→
   190→    thinking_time = scene.thinking_time
   191→    if thinking_time > 2.0:
   192→        time.sleep(thinking_time * 0.4)
   193→        face2 = random.choice(_get_faces(ctx))
   194→        verb2 = random.choice(_get_verbs(ctx))
   195→        spinner.update(f"{face2} {verb2}...")
   196→        time.sleep(thinking_time * 0.6)
   197→    else:
   198→        time.sleep(thinking_time)
   199→
   200→    spinner.stop()
   201→    ctx.mark(scene_timing, "thinking_end")
   202→
   203→    # --- Tool progress lines ---
   204→    if scene.tools:
   205→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "tools_start")
   206→        for tool in scene.tools:
   207→            tool_dict = tool.model_dump() if hasattr(tool, "model_dump") else tool
   208→            show_tool_line(tool_dict, ctx)
   209→            tool_delay = tool_dict.get("delay", 0.15)
   210→            time.sleep(tool_delay)
   211→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "tools_end")
   212→
   213→    # --- Response ---
   214→    if scene.response:
   215→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "response_start")
   216→        show_response(scene.response, label=response_label, ctx=ctx)
   217→        ctx.mark(scene_timing, "response_end")
   218→
   219→    time.sleep(scene.post_pause)
   220→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
   221→
   222→    if ctx.timing:
   223→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
   224→
```

```
1→"""Action scenes: clear, pause, print, banner, type_command, camera."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import sys
     7→import time
     8→
     9→from rich.panel import Panel
    10→
    11→from demo.schema import ActionScene
    12→from demo.scenes import (
    13→    PlaybackContext, SceneTiming, _BOLD, _DIM, _RST,
    14→    hex_to_ansi, register,
    15→)
    16→from demo.scenes.conversation import type_text
    17→
    18→
    19→# ── Generic action dispatcher ──────────────────────────────────────────
    20→
    21→@register("action")
    22→def play_action(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    23→    """Dispatch an action scene to the right sub-handler."""
    24→    handlers = {
    25→        "pause": _action_pause,
    26→        "clear": _action_clear,
    27→        "print": _action_print,
    28→        "banner": _action_banner,
    29→        "type_command": _action_type_command,
    30→        "camera": _action_camera,
    31→    }
    32→    handler = handlers.get(scene.action)
    33→    if handler:
    34→        handler(scene, ctx)
    35→
    36→
    37→# ── Individual action handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────
    38→
    39→def _action_pause(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    40→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    41→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="pause"
    42→    )
    43→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    44→    time.sleep(scene.duration or 1.0)
    45→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
    46→    if ctx.timing:
    47→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
    48→
    49→
    50→def _action_clear(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    51→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    52→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="clear"
    53→    )
    54→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    55→    os.system("clear")
    56→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
    57→    if ctx.timing:
    58→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _action_print(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    62→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    63→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="print"
    64→    )
    65→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    66→    text = scene.text or ""
    67→    if scene.color:
    68→        ctx.console.print(f"[{scene.color}]{text}[/]")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(text)
    71→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
    72→    if ctx.timing:
    73→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
    74→
    75→
    76→def _action_type_command(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
    77→    """Type a raw shell command (not the agent prompt)."""
    78→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
    79→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="type_command"
    80→    )
    81→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
    82→
    83→    cmd = scene.command or ""
    84→    prefix = scene.prefix or "$ "
    85→
    86→    sys.stdout.write(f"\n{_DIM}{prefix}{_RST}")
    87→    sys.stdout.flush()
    88→    time.sleep(0.3)
    89→    type_text(cmd, speed=ctx.typing_speed, color=f"{_BOLD}")
    90→    time.sleep(0.5)
    91→    sys.stdout.write("\n")
    92→    sys.stdout.flush()
    93→
    94→    output = scene.output or ""
    95→    if output:
    96→        for line in output.split("\n"):
    97→            sys.stdout.write(f"{_DIM}{line}{_RST}\n")
    98→            sys.stdout.flush()
    99→            time.sleep(0.05)
   100→
   101→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
   102→    if ctx.timing:
   103→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
   104→
   105→
   106→def _action_camera(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   107→    """Standalone camera directive — only records timing, no visual output."""
   108→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
   109→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="camera"
   110→    )
   111→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
   112→    scene_timing.markers["camera"] = ctx.now()
   113→    # Store camera params in markers for the camera resolver
   114→    if scene.zoom is not None:
   115→        scene_timing.markers["_zoom"] = scene.zoom
   116→    if scene.x is not None:
   117→        scene_timing.markers["_x"] = scene.x
   118→    if scene.y is not None:
   119→        scene_timing.markers["_y"] = scene.y
   120→    if scene.duration is not None:
   121→        scene_timing.markers["_duration"] = scene.duration
   122→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
   123→    if ctx.timing:
   124→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
   125→
   126→
   127→# ── Banner ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   128→
   129→def _action_banner(scene: ActionScene, ctx: PlaybackContext):
   130→    """Print the Hermes welcome banner using skin colors."""
   131→    from rich.table import Table
   132→
   133→    scene_timing = SceneTiming(
   134→        index=ctx.scene_index, scene_type="action", action="banner"
   135→    )
   136→    scene_timing.start_t = ctx.now()
   137→
   138→    model = scene.model or "nous-hermes-3"
   139→    context_str = scene.context or "128K"
   140→    session_id = scene.session_id or "demo-session"
   141→    tools_count = scene.tools_count or 24
   142→    skills_count = scene.skills_count or 42
   143→
   144→    # Color references from skin
   145→    c_border = ctx.skin.get_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32")
   146→    c_title = ctx.skin.get_color("banner_title", "#FFD700")
   147→    c_accent = ctx.skin.get_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00")
   148→    c_dim = ctx.skin.get_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B")
   149→    c_text = ctx.skin.get_color("banner_text", "#FFF8DC")
   150→
   151→    # Use skin's custom banner art if available, else default
   152→    agent_name = ctx.skin.get_branding("agent_name", "Hermes Agent")
   153→
   154→    hero = ctx.skin.banner_hero or _default_caduceus(c_border, c_accent, c_title, c_dim)
   155→    logo = ctx.skin.banner_logo or _default_logo(c_title, c_accent, c_border)
   156→
   157→    layout = Table.grid(padding=(0, 2))
   158→    layout.add_column("left", justify="center")
   159→    layout.add_column("right", justify="left")
   160→
   161→    left = (
   162→        f"\n{hero}\n\n"
   163→        f"[{c_accent}]{model}[/] [dim {c_dim}]\u00b7[/] "
   164→        f"[dim {c_dim}]{context_str} context[/] [dim {c_dim}]\u00b7[/] "
   165→        f"[dim {c_dim}]Nous Research[/]\n"
   166→        f"[dim {c_dim}]Session: {session_id}[/]"
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    right_lines = [
   170→        f"[bold {c_accent}]Available Tools[/]",
   171→        f"[dim {c_dim}]core_tools:[/] [{c_text}]terminal, read_file, write_file, patch, search_files[/]",
   172→        f"[dim {c_dim}]web_tools:[/] [{c_text}]web_search, web_extract, web_crawl[/]",
   173→        f"[dim {c_dim}]browser_tools:[/] [{c_text}]browser_navigate, browser_click, browser_type, ...[/]",
   174→        f"[dim {c_dim}]agent_tools:[/] [{c_text}]delegate_task, mixture_of_agents, memory[/]",
   175→        "",
   176→        f"[bold {c_accent}]Available Skills[/]",
   177→        f"[dim {c_dim}]productivity:[/] [{c_text}]email-send, web-search, summarize, ...[/]",
   178→        f"[dim {c_dim}]development:[/] [{c_text}]git-commit, code-review, test-runner, ...[/]",
   179→        "",
   180→        f"[dim {c_dim}]{tools_count} tools \u00b7 {skills_count} skills \u00b7 /help for commands[/]",
   181→    ]
   182→    right = "\n".join(right_lines)
   183→    layout.add_row(left, right)
   184→
   185→    ctx.console.print()
   186→    ctx.console.print(logo)
   187→    ctx.console.print()
   188→    ctx.console.print(Panel(
   189→        layout,
   190→        title=f"[bold {c_title}]{agent_name} v0.9.0[/]",
   191→        border_style=c_border,
   192→        padding=(0, 2),
   193→    ))
   194→
   195→    scene_timing.end_t = ctx.now()
   196→    if ctx.timing:
   197→        ctx.timing.add_scene(scene_timing)
   198→
   199→
   200→# ── Default banner art (Hermes caduceus + logo) ────────────────────────
   201→
   202→def _default_caduceus(c1: str, c2: str, c3: str, c4: str) -> str:
   203→    """Default Hermes caduceus art with 4-color gradient.
   204→
   205→    Colors map: c1=border, c2=accent, c3=title, c4=dim
   206→    Original uses: #CD7F32, #FFBF00, #FFD700, #B8860B
   207→    """
   208→    return (
   209→        f"[{c1}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣀⡀⠀⣀⣀⠀⢀⣀⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   210→        f"[{c1}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣠⣴⣾⣿⣿⣇⠸⣿⣿⠇⣸⣿⣿⣷⣦⣄⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   211→        f"[{c2}]⠀⢀⣠⣴⣶⠿⠋⣩⡿⣿⡿⠻⣿⡇⢠⡄⢸⣿⠟⢿⣿⢿⣍⠙⠿⣶⣦⣄⡀⠀[/]\n"
   212→        f"[{c2}]⠀⠀⠉⠉⠁⠶⠟⠋⠀⠉⠀⢀⣈⣁⡈⢁⣈⣁⡀⠀⠉⠀⠙⠻⠶⠈⠉⠉⠀⠀[/]\n"
   213→        f"[{c3}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣴⣿⡿⠛⢁⡈⠛⢿⣿⣦⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   214→        f"[{c3}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠿⣿⣦⣤⣈⠁⢠⣴⣿⠿⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   215→        f"[{c2}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠉⠻⢿⣿⣦⡉⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   216→        f"[{c2}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠘⢷⣦⣈⠛⠃⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   217→        f"[{c1}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢠⣴⠦⠈⠙⠿⣦⡄⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   218→        f"[{c1}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠸⣿⣤⡈⠁⢤⣿⠇⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   219→        f"[{c4}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠉⠛⠷⠄⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   220→        f"[{c4}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣀⠑⢶⣄⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   221→        f"[{c4}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣿⠁⢰⡆⠈⡿⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   222→        f"[{c4}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠳⠈⣡⠞⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]\n"
   223→        f"[{c4}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]"
   224→    )
   225→
   226→
   227→def _default_logo(c1: str, c2: str, c3: str) -> str:
   228→    """Default HERMES AGENT block logo."""
```

```
    98→[{dim}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣿⠁⢰⡆⠈⡿⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
    99→[{dim}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠳⠈⣡⠞⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   100→[{dim}]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]"""
   101→
   102→
   103→def _build_compact_banner() -> str:
   104→    """Build the compact banner using active skin colors."""
   105→    title = _skin_color("banner_title", "#FFD700")
   106→    accent = _skin_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00")
   107→    border = _skin_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32")
   108→    dim = _skin_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B")
   109→    return f"""
   110→[bold {title}]╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗[/]
   111→[bold {title}]║[/]  [{accent}]⚕ NOUS HERMES[/] [dim {dim}]- AI Agent Framework[/]              [bold {title}]║[/]
   112→[bold {title}]║[/]  [{border}]Messenger of the Digital Gods[/]    [dim {dim}]Nous Research[/]   [bold {title}]║[/]
   113→[bold {title}]╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝[/]
   114→"""
   115→
   116→
   117→# Backward-compatible aliases (now dynamic)
   118→HERMES_AGENT_LOGO = None  # Use _build_hermes_logo() instead
   119→HERMES_CADUCEUS = None    # Use _build_hermes_caduceus() instead
   120→COMPACT_BANNER = None     # Use _build_compact_banner() instead
   121→
   122→
   123→# =========================================================================
   124→# Skills scanning
   125→# =========================================================================
   126→
   127→def get_available_skills() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   128→    """Scan ~/.hermes/skills/ and return skills grouped by category."""
   129→    import os
   130→
   131→    hermes_home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   132→    skills_dir = hermes_home / "skills"
   133→    skills_by_category = {}
   134→
   135→    if not skills_dir.exists():
   136→        return skills_by_category
   137→
   138→    for skill_file in skills_dir.rglob("SKILL.md"):
   139→        rel_path = skill_file.relative_to(skills_dir)
   140→        parts = rel_path.parts
   141→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   142→            category = parts[0]
   143→            skill_name = parts[-2]
   144→        else:
   145→            category = "general"
   146→            skill_name = skill_file.parent.name
   147→        skills_by_category.setdefault(category, []).append(skill_name)
   148→
   149→    return skills_by_category
   150→
   151→
   152→# =========================================================================
   153→# Update check
   154→# =========================================================================
   155→
   156→# Cache update check results for 6 hours to avoid repeated git fetches
   157→_UPDATE_CHECK_CACHE_SECONDS = 6 * 3600
   158→
   159→
   160→def check_for_updates() -> Optional[int]:
   161→    """Check how many commits behind origin/main the local repo is.
   162→
   163→    Does a ``git fetch`` at most once every 6 hours (cached to
   164→    ``~/.hermes/.update_check``).  Returns the number of commits behind,
   165→    or ``None`` if the check fails or isn't applicable.
   166→    """
   167→    hermes_home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   168→    repo_dir = hermes_home / "hermes-agent"
   169→    cache_file = hermes_home / ".update_check"
   170→
   171→    # Must be a git repo
   172→    if not (repo_dir / ".git").exists():
   173→        return None
   174→
   175→    # Read cache
   176→    now = time.time()
   177→    try:
   178→        if cache_file.exists():
   179→            cached = json.loads(cache_file.read_text())
   180→            if now - cached.get("ts", 0) < _UPDATE_CHECK_CACHE_SECONDS:
   181→                return cached.get("behind")
   182→    except Exception:
   183→        pass
   184→
   185→    # Fetch latest refs (fast — only downloads ref metadata, no files)
   186→    try:
   187→        subprocess.run(
   188→            ["git", "fetch", "origin", "--quiet"],
   189→            capture_output=True, timeout=10,
   190→            cwd=str(repo_dir),
   191→        )
   192→    except Exception:
   193→        pass  # Offline or timeout — use stale refs, that's fine
   194→
   195→    # Count commits behind
   196→    try:
   197→        result = subprocess.run(
   198→            ["git", "rev-list", "--count", "HEAD..origin/main"],
   199→            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5,
   200→            cwd=str(repo_dir),
   201→        )
   202→        if result.returncode == 0:
   203→            behind = int(result.stdout.strip())
   204→        else:
   205→            behind = None
   206→    except Exception:
   207→        behind = None
   208→
   209→    # Write cache
   210→    try:
   211→        cache_file.write_text(json.dumps({"ts": now, "behind": behind}))
   212→    except Exception:
   213→        pass
   214→
   215→    return behind
   216→
   217→
   218→# =========================================================================
   219→# Welcome banner
   220→# =========================================================================
   221→
   222→def _format_context_length(tokens: int) -> str:
   223→    """Format a token count for display (e.g. 128000 → '128K', 1048576 → '1M')."""
   224→    if tokens >= 1_000_000:
   225→        val = tokens / 1_000_000
   226→        return f"{val:g}M"
   227→    elif tokens >= 1_000:
   228→        val = tokens / 1_000
   229→        return f"{val:g}K"
   230→    return str(tokens)
   231→
   232→
   233→def build_welcome_banner(console: Console, model: str, cwd: str,
   234→                         tools: List[dict] = None,
   235→                         enabled_toolsets: List[str] = None,
   236→                         session_id: str = None,
   237→                         get_toolset_for_tool=None,
   238→                         context_length: int = None):
   239→    """Build and print a welcome banner with caduceus on left and info on right.
   240→
   241→    Args:
   242→        console: Rich Console instance.
   243→        model: Current model name.
   244→        cwd: Current working directory.
   245→        tools: List of tool definitions.
   246→        enabled_toolsets: List of enabled toolset names.
   247→        session_id: Session identifier.
   248→        get_toolset_for_tool: Callable to map tool name -> toolset name.
   249→        context_length: Model's context window size in tokens.
   250→    """
   251→    from model_tools import check_tool_availability
   252→    if get_toolset_for_tool is None:
   253→        from model_tools import get_toolset_for_tool
   254→
   255→    tools = tools or []
   256→    enabled_toolsets = enabled_toolsets or []
   257→
   258→    _, unavailable_toolsets = check_tool_availability(quiet=True)
   259→    disabled_tools = set()
   260→    for item in unavailable_toolsets:
   261→        disabled_tools.update(item.get("tools", []))
   262→
   263→    layout_table = Table.grid(padding=(0, 2))
   264→    layout_table.add_column("left", justify="center")
   265→    layout_table.add_column("right", justify="left")
   266→
   267→    # Resolve skin colors once for the entire banner
   268→    accent = _skin_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00")
   269→    dim = _skin_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B")
   270→    text = _skin_color("banner_text", "#FFF8DC")
   271→    session_color = _skin_color("session_border", "#8B8682")
   272→
   273→
   274→    # Use skin's banner_hero if available, else dynamic caduceus
   275→    try:
   276→        from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin
   277→        _hero = get_active_skin().banner_hero
   278→    except Exception:
   279→        _hero = ""
   280→    left_lines = ["", _hero or _build_hermes_caduceus(), ""]
   281→    model_short = model.split("/")[-1] if "/" in model else model
   282→    if len(model_short) > 28:
   283→        model_short = model_short[:25] + "..."
   284→    ctx_str = f" [dim {dim}]·[/] [dim {dim}]{_format_context_length(context_length)} context[/]" if context_length else ""
   285→    left_lines.append(f"[{accent}]{model_short}[/]{ctx_str} [dim {dim}]·[/] [dim {dim}]Nous Research[/]")
   286→    left_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{cwd}[/]")
   287→    if session_id:
   288→        left_lines.append(f"[dim {session_color}]Session: {session_id}[/]")
   289→    left_content = "\n".join(left_lines)
   290→
   291→    right_lines = [f"[bold {accent}]Available Tools[/]"]
   292→    toolsets_dict: Dict[str, list] = {}
   293→
   294→    for tool in tools:
   295→        tool_name = tool["function"]["name"]
   296→        toolset = get_toolset_for_tool(tool_name) or "other"
   297→        toolsets_dict.setdefault(toolset, []).append(tool_name)
   298→
   299→    for item in unavailable_toolsets:
   300→        toolset_id = item.get("id", item.get("name", "unknown"))
   301→        display_name = f"{toolset_id}_tools" if not toolset_id.endswith("_tools") else toolset_id
   302→        if display_name not in toolsets_dict:
   303→            toolsets_dict[display_name] = []
   304→        for tool_name in item.get("tools", []):
   305→            if tool_name not in toolsets_dict[display_name]:
   306→                toolsets_dict[display_name].append(tool_name)
   307→
   308→    sorted_toolsets = sorted(toolsets_dict.keys())
   309→    display_toolsets = sorted_toolsets[:8]
   310→    remaining_toolsets = len(sorted_toolsets) - 8
   311→
   312→    for toolset in display_toolsets:
   313→        tool_names = toolsets_dict[toolset]
   314→        colored_names = []
   315→        for name in sorted(tool_names):
   316→            if name in disabled_tools:
   317→                colored_names.append(f"[red]{name}[/]")
   318→            else:
   319→                colored_names.append(f"[{text}]{name}[/]")
   320→
   321→        tools_str = ", ".join(colored_names)
   322→        if len(", ".join(sorted(tool_names))) > 45:
   323→            short_names = []
   324→            length = 0
   325→            for name in sorted(tool_names):
   326→                if length + len(name) + 2 > 42:
   327→                    short_names.append("...")
   328→                    break
   329→                short_names.append(name)
   330→                length += len(name) + 2
   331→            colored_names = []
   332→            for name in short_names:
   333→                if name == "...":
   334→                    colored_names.append("[dim]...[/]")
   335→                elif name in disabled_tools:
   336→                    colored_names.append(f"[red]{name}[/]")
   337→                else:
   338→                    colored_names.append(f"[{text}]{name}[/]")
   339→            tools_str = ", ".join(colored_names)
   340→
   341→        right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{toolset}:[/] {tools_str}")
   342→
   343→    if remaining_toolsets > 0:
   344→        right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}](and {remaining_toolsets} more toolsets...)[/]")
   345→
   346→    # MCP Servers section (only if configured)
   347→    try:
   348→        from tools.mcp_tool import get_mcp_status
   349→        mcp_status = get_mcp_status()
   350→    except Exception:
   351→        mcp_status = []
   352→
   353→    if mcp_status:
   354→        right_lines.append("")
   355→        right_lines.append(f"[bold {accent}]MCP Servers[/]")
   356→        for srv in mcp_status:
   357→            if srv["connected"]:
   358→                right_lines.append(
   359→                    f"[dim {dim}]{srv['name']}[/] [{text}]({srv['transport']})[/] "
   360→                    f"[dim {dim}]—[/] [{text}]{srv['tools']} tool(s)[/]"
   361→                )
   362→            else:
   363→                right_lines.append(
   364→                    f"[red]{srv['name']}[/] [dim]({srv['transport']})[/] "
   365→                    f"[red]— failed[/]"
   366→                )
   367→
   368→    right_lines.append("")
   369→    right_lines.append(f"[bold {accent}]Available Skills[/]")
   370→    skills_by_category = get_available_skills()
   371→    total_skills = sum(len(s) for s in skills_by_category.values())
   372→
   373→    if skills_by_category:
   374→        for category in sorted(skills_by_category.keys()):
   375→            skill_names = sorted(skills_by_category[category])
   376→            if len(skill_names) > 8:
   377→                display_names = skill_names[:8]
   378→                skills_str = ", ".join(display_names) + f" +{len(skill_names) - 8} more"
   379→            else:
   380→                skills_str = ", ".join(skill_names)
   381→            if len(skills_str) > 50:
   382→                skills_str = skills_str[:47] + "..."
   383→            right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{category}:[/] [{text}]{skills_str}[/]")
   384→    else:
   385→        right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]No skills installed[/]")
   386→
   387→    right_lines.append("")
   388→    mcp_connected = sum(1 for s in mcp_status if s["connected"]) if mcp_status else 0
   389→    summary_parts = [f"{len(tools)} tools", f"{total_skills} skills"]
   390→    if mcp_connected:
   391→        summary_parts.append(f"{mcp_connected} MCP servers")
   392→    summary_parts.append("/help for commands")
   393→    right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{' · '.join(summary_parts)}[/]")
   394→
   395→    # Update check — show if behind origin/main
   396→    try:
   397→        behind = check_for_updates()
   398→        if behind and behind > 0:
   399→            commits_word = "commit" if behind == 1 else "commits"
   400→            right_lines.append(
   401→                f"[bold yellow]⚠ {behind} {commits_word} behind[/]"
   402→                f"[dim yellow] — run [bold]hermes update[/bold] to update[/]"
   403→            )
   404→    except Exception:
   405→        pass  # Never break the banner over an update check
   406→
   407→    right_content = "\n".join(right_lines)
   408→    layout_table.add_row(left_content, right_content)
   409→
   410→    agent_name = _skin_branding("agent_name", "Hermes Agent")
   411→    title_color = _skin_color("banner_title", "#FFD700")
   412→    border_color = _skin_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32")
   413→    outer_panel = Panel(
   414→        layout_table,
   415→        title=f"[bold {title_color}]{agent_name} v{VERSION} ({RELEASE_DATE})[/]",
   416→        border_style=border_color,
   417→        padding=(0, 2),
   418→    )
   419→
   420→    # Use skin's banner_logo if available, else dynamic logo
   421→    try:
   422→        from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin
   423→        _logo = get_active_skin().banner_logo
   424→    except Exception:
   425→        _logo = ""
   426→
   427→    console.print()
   428→    console.print(_logo or _build_hermes_logo())
   429→    console.print()
   430→    console.print(outer_panel)
   431→
```
